(iv) NCTB-SciGen8, a curriculum-grounded dataset of 198 items (143 multiple-choice, 55 creative questions) spanning all 14 chapters of the NCTB Class 8 science textbook, produced by the pipeline and rated by three practicing teachers. TeachMateGPT raises faithfulness (0.68 $\rightarrow$ 0.96) and answer relevancy (0.60 $\rightarrow$ 0.89) over a vanilla RAG baseline.

</details>


### [78] [StreamHear: Domain-Adapted Pseudo-Labeling for Semi-Supervised Streaming Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13717)
*Zefang Liu,Chenyang Zhu,Sangwoo Cho,Xujun Peng,Shi-Xiong Zhang,Sambit Sahu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: StreamHear是一种半监督流水线，通过离线教师模型生成伪标签并结合先验正则化的动态规划重对齐步骤，将流式ASR学生模型适应到域偏移的目标音频，在四个数据集上优于监督微调并缩小了与离线教师的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式ASR在域偏移的目标音频上表现不佳，而有标注的域内数据成本高昂，无标注音频却大量可用，因此需要一种利用无标注数据的半监督适应方法。

Method: 提出StreamHear半监督流水线：(1) 在标注训练集上微调离线transducer教师模型；(2) 用教师模型在无标注数据上生成伪标签；(3) 在有标注数据和伪标签数据的混合集上微调流式学生模型；(4) 引入先验正则化的动态规划重对齐步骤，利用ASR假设锚点修正块级词位置。

Result: 在金融电话、预备朗读语音和电话质量对话四个数据集上，StreamHear一致优于监督学生微调，并缩小了流式学生与离线教师之间的性能差距。

Conclusion: StreamHear通过半监督伪标签和重对齐策略，有效利用无标注数据实现流式ASR的域适应，是一种低成本高性能的解决方案。

Abstract: Streaming automatic speech recognition (ASR) underperforms on domain-shifted target audio, where labeled in-domain data is costly to prepare while unlabeled audio is abundant. We present StreamHear, a semi-supervised pipeline that adapts a pretrained streaming student by fine-tuning an offline transducer teacher on the labeled training set, generating pseudo-labels on the unlabeled portion, and fine-tuning the student on the mixture. We further introduce a prior-regularized dynamic-programming realignment step that fixes chunk-level word placement using an ASR-hypothesis anchor. Across four datasets spanning financial calls, prepared read speech, and phone-quality dialogue, StreamHear consistently outperforms supervised student fine-tuning and narrows the gap to the offline teacher.

</details>


### [79] [BM25-Augmented Many-Shot Translation for Low-Resource North-Eastern Indian Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13722)
*Aashish Dhawan,Christopher Driggers-Ellis,Dzmitry Kasinets,Christan Grant,Daisy Zhe Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UF Gators submission to WMT26 uses retrieval-augmented many-shot translation: BM25 retrieves similar parallel examples from training banks (WMT26 + Samanantar + prior WMT data), then Gemini 2.5 Flash translates conditioned on these examples without fine-tuning. Grid search over retrieval/example counts selects best config for each of 22 English↔11 North-Eastern Indian language pairs.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To address low-resource Indic language translation by leveraging retrieval-augmented many-shot prompting with a large language model (Gemini 2.5 Flash) without requiring expensive fine-tuning, adapting a pipeline previously used for AmericasNLP to the WMT26 Low-Resource Indic Language Translation task.

Method: Pipeline: (1) Build language-specific training banks combining official WMT26 data, Samanantar corpus, and prior WMT shared task releases. (2) At inference, use BM25 to retrieve r most similar parallel examples from the training bank. (3) Feed retrieved examples as exemplars (d development exemplars selected from retrieved set) along with the source sentence to Gemini 2.5 Flash for translation. (4) No model fine-tuning. (5) Perform grid search over retrieval count r and development exemplar count d across all 22 language-direction pairs to select optimal configuration per submission.

Result: Grid search over r and d across 22 language-direction pairs identifies best configuration for each submission. Results are presumably competitive for low-resource Indic language translation without any fine-tuning, demonstrating effectiveness of retrieval-augmented many-shot prompting.

Conclusion: Retrieval-augmented many-shot translation using Gemini 2.5 Flash, without fine-tuning, can effectively handle low-resource Indic language translation across 11 North-Eastern Indian languages in both directions. Combining multiple public corpora and tuning retrieval/example counts per language pair is key to performance.

Abstract: This paper describes the University of Florida Gators submission to the WMT26 Low-Resource Indic Language Translation shared task. We adapt the retrieval-augmented many-shot translation pipeline from our AmericasNLP 2026 system to translate between English and eleven North-Eastern Indian languages in both directions. At inference time, BM25 retrieves the most similar parallel examples from a language-specific training bank, and Gemini 2.5 Flash translates the input conditioned on these examples. No model fine-tuning is involved. Training banks combine official WMT26 data with publicly available corpora such as Samanantar and prior WMT shared task releases. A grid search over retrieval count r and development exemplar count d across all 22 language-direction pairs selects the best configuration for each submission.

</details>


### [80] [GALA: Generation-Aware Cross-Modal Alignment for Text-to-Time-Series Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13741)
*Haochen Zhang,Gengwei Zhang,Laura Yao,Nicholas Knoz,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GALA 是一种面向文本条件时间序列生成的两阶段方法，通过对比学习与生成式辅助损失将文本编码器与时间序列基础模型对齐到共享嵌入空间，再利用对齐后的文本嵌入驱动流匹配生成器，在 TSFragment-600K 上实现了 30/36 个指标第一，平均排名 1.08/1.08/1.42（长度 24/48/96），并打破了生成器内部文本编码器带来的保真度与文本遵循度之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本条件时间序列生成器要么直接使用冻结的通用文本编码器嵌入，要么端到端调整编码器但仅将去噪损失作为副产物来塑造嵌入，两种方式均未有意地将条件表示与信号模态对齐，导致条件表示不适合指导生成过程。

Method: GALA 采用两阶段流程：第一阶段通过对比学习将预训练文本编码器与时间序列基础模型耦合到共享嵌入空间，并引入生成式辅助损失使两个编码器适应生成任务；第二阶段冻结对齐后的文本嵌入，用于驱动流匹配生成器。

Result: 在 TSFragment-600K 数据集（涵盖四个领域和三种片段长度）上，GALA 在 36 个指标列中排名第一 30 次，在长度 24/48/96 下的平均排名分别为 1.08/1.08/1.42，而最强基线为 1.92/2.00/1.75。消融实验表明，生成式辅助损失是对齐的必要组件而非附加功能，去除后 FID、CTTP 和 JFTSD 同时退化。

Conclusion: GALA 通过生成感知的跨模态对齐，有效解决了文本条件时间序列生成中条件表示与信号模态不匹配的问题，打破了保真度与文本遵循度之间的权衡，表明生成式损失是对齐过程不可或缺的组成部分。

Abstract: Synthesizing time series from natural language is emerging as the most expressive form of controllable time series generation. However, existing text-conditioned generators either take caption embeddings frozen from off-the-shelf text encoders, or adapt the encoder end-to-end, letting the denoising loss shape the embeddings only as a by-product. In either case, the conditioning representation is never deliberately matched to the signal modality, leaving it ill-suited to guide generation. We address this by introducing GALA: Generation-Aware cross-modaL Alignment for text conditional time series generation. GALA is a two-stage approach that first contrastively couples a pretrained text encoder with a time-series foundation model into a shared embedding space with both encoders adapted to generation by an auxiliary generative loss, and then freezes the resulting caption embedding to drive a flow-matching generator. On TSFragment-600K, spanning four domains and three fragment lengths, GALA sets a new state of the art, ranking first in 30 of 36 metric columns and reaching an average rank of 1.08/1.08/1.42 at lengths 24/48/96 against 1.92/2.00/1.75 for the strongest baseline. We further find that generator-internal text encoders force a trade-off between fidelity and caption adherence, whereas conditioning on the aligned embedding breaks it: FID, CTTP, and JFTSD all improve at once. Ablating the auxiliary loss degrades FID, CTTP and JFTSD together, it indicates the generative term is a necessary component of the alignment rather than an add-on.

</details>


### [81] [Amplified Does Not Mean Predictive: Reasoning Behaviors in Thinking Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13760)
*Jean de Dieu Nyandwi,Leena Mathur,Yonatan Bisk,Robert Hawkins,Graham Neubig*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了推理模型中哪些推理行为与正确答案相关，以及面向推理的训练是否放大了这些行为。通过引入“行为提升”（Behavioral Lift）指标，量化了行为存在与缺失时正确率的变化。在15个模型和6个基准测试（涵盖纯文本和视觉-语言推理）中，对15,282条推理轨迹进行了标注。发现存在“放大-提升差距”：思维模型强烈放大自我修正、假设检验和不确定性承认，而提升最高的行为是置信度校准、知识对齐和自我意识。置信度校正是两个模态中最强的正确信号之一，但几乎未被放大；不确定性承认被放大3-7倍，但与正确性关联弱或负相关。推理导向的训练并未优先放大提升最高的行为，这激励了奖励校准和 grounded 推理的过程级目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分推理行为与正确性的关联，以及推理导向训练是否真正放大了那些最有助于正确性的行为。现有研究可能高估了某些表面推理行为（如自我修正）的价值，而忽视了真正关键的行为（如置信度校准）。

Method: 提出 Behavioral Lift 指标，量化行为存在与缺失时模型正确率的变化。在15个模型（含文本和视觉-语言推理模型）和6个基准上，依据自定义分类法对15,282条推理轨迹进行标注，分析各行为的出现频率（放大程度）与其对正确性的提升（Lift）之间的差异。

Result: 发现 Amplification-Lift Gap：思维模型强烈放大自我修正、假设检验和不确定性承认，但这些行为对正确性的提升并不高；而提升最高的行为（置信度校准、知识对齐、自我意识）却未被优先放大。置信度校准在两个模态中都是最强正面信号之一，但几乎未被放大；不确定性承认被放大3-7倍，却与正确性弱相关或负相关。

Conclusion: 推理导向的训练并未优先放大与正确性最相关的行为，因此需要设计过程级目标来奖励校准和 grounded 的推理，而非仅关注表面形式。

Abstract: Which reasoning behaviors are associated with correct answers in reasoning models, and does reasoning-oriented training amplify those behaviors? This distinction is important because reasoning-oriented training can make traces look more deliberative without amplifying the behaviors most tied to model correctness. We quantify this mismatch with Behavioral Lift, a metric that measures how much correctness changes when a behavior is present versus absent in a model's reasoning trace. Across 15 models and 6 benchmarks spanning text-only and vision-language reasoning, we annotate 15,282 traces with a taxonomy whose core behaviors are defined for both LLM and VLM traces. We find evidence for an Amplification-Lift Gap, in which thinking models strongly amplify self-correction, hypothesis testing, and uncertainty acknowledgment, while the highest-lift behaviors are confidence calibration, knowledge alignment, and self-awareness. Confidence calibration is among the strongest positive signals of correctness in both modalities, yet is barely amplified; uncertainty acknowledgment is amplified by 3--7$\times$, yet is weakly or negatively associated with correctness. We find that reasoning-oriented training does not preferentially amplify the highest-Lift behaviors, motivating process-level objectives that reward calibrated and grounded reasoning rather than surface form alone.

</details>


### [82] [When Lexical Change Misleads: Rethinking Dynamic Topic Model Evaluation with Traditional and LLM-Based Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13835)
*Charu Karakkaparambil James*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 传统主题连贯性指标在动态主题模型中因词汇变化而失效，本文通过人工评估和LLM语义相似度对比，发现两种指标互补，建议按词汇变化程度分层评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态主题模型的词汇分布随时间演变，传统连贯性指标在词汇变化而语义不变时无法准确反映主题质量，需要更有效的评估方法。

Method: 从CoNTM和DLDA两个模型在NYT、DBLP、arXiv三个数据集上提取120个主题，分为低、中、高三种词汇变化类别，由三名人工标注者评估，同时计算传统时间连贯性和LLM语义相似度，对比其与人工判断的一致性。

Result: 传统时间连贯性与人工判断的一致性波动极大（ρ=-0.256~0.614）；LLM语义相似度对CoNTM与人工判断高度一致（NYT ρ=0.609，DBLP ρ=0.721，arXiv ρ=0.502），但对DLDA一致性较差；词汇变化分层揭示了聚合评估掩盖的差异。

Conclusion: 应采用词汇变化感知的评估方式，将传统连贯性与LLM语义度量作为互补信号联合报告，而非相互替代。

Abstract: Dynamic topic models capture evolving word distributions, but traditional coherence metrics may fail when vocabulary changes while semantic meaning persists. We evaluate 120 topics from CoNTM and DLDA across NYT, DBLP, and arXiv, using three human annotators and Low, Medium, and High lexical-change categories. Traditional temporal coherence shows highly variable agreement with human judgments ($ρ$=-0.256 to 0.614). In contrast, LLM-based semantic similarity agrees strongly with human semantic judgments for CoNTM on NYT ($ρ$=0.609), DBLP ($ρ$=0.721), and arXiv ($ρ$=0.502), but is less consistent for DLDA. Lexical-change stratification reveals variation hidden by aggregate evaluation. We therefore advocate lexical-change-aware evaluation, jointly reporting traditional coherence and LLM-based semantic measures as complementary rather than interchangeable signals.

</details>


### [83] [ASSERT: A Measurement Pipeline for GenAI Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13840)
*Riccardo Fogliato,Abhinav Palia,Xiawei Wang,Emily Sheng,Chad Atalla,Jean Garcia-Gathright,Nicholas Pangakis,Sharman Tan,Dan Vann,Hannah Washington,P. Alex Dow,Heba Elfardy,Hanna Wallach,Sandeep Atluri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ASSERT，一种基于规范驱动的生成式AI审计测量流程，将每次报告的行为率与明确测量规范绑定，解决因测量选择变化导致报告率变动难以归因的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式AI审计报告的行为率受测量选择（如对话设置、模拟用户、评判标准等）影响，导致率值变化时无法区分是系统行为改变还是测量方法变化所致，阻碍了跨系统比较、回归追踪和部署决策。

Method: ASSERT流程包括：编写行为评分标准和测试用例的规范，然后对生成式AI系统执行审计，最终返回一个与明确测量规范绑定的报告率。通过案例研究（对话欺骗）展示不同测量选择如何影响报告率和系统排名。

Result: 案例研究表明，对话设置、模拟用户、评判者和不合规证据门槛等测量选择会显著改变报告率，并可能重新排序生成式AI系统的排名。将每次报告率与明确规范绑定，有助于归因和解释跨审计差异。

Conclusion: ASSERT通过规范驱动的测量流程，增强了生成式AI审计的透明性和可归因性，使报告率变化更容易被理解和解释，为更可靠的系统比较和部署决策提供支持。

Abstract: Audits of generative AI (GenAI) systems often summarize behavior as a reported rate: how often the audited system complies with policy. Researchers and stakeholders use that rate to compare systems, track regressions, and gate deployment. A reported rate reflects both the system under audit and the measurement choices behind it, so a change in the rate can leave it unclear whether the system or those choices moved. We introduce ASSERT, a specification-driven measurement pipeline for GenAI audits that ties each reported rate to a written specification of the measurement choices used to produce it. ASSERT helps draft a behavioral rubric and test cases, then runs the audit against a GenAI system and returns a reported rate. In a case study on conversational deception, we observe that the reported rate moves substantially with the dialogue setup, the simulated user, the judge, and the evidence bar for non-compliance. These measurement choices substantially change the reported rate and can reorder GenAI system rankings. Because each reported rate is tied to an explicit specification, differences across audits are easier to attribute and interpret.

</details>


### [84] [Bootstrapping Niche Multilingual Code Translation via Reinforcement Learning with Execution-Based Verifiable Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13854)
*Kouki Yuki,Jie Zeng,Kyoko Ogawa,Ryunosuke Ikeda,Yohei Kobashi,Takeshi Kojima,Ikuya Yamada,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于执行监督的偏好强化学习框架，用于解决多语言代码翻译中平行语料稀疏、翻译结果无法执行的问题。通过扩展已验证的Python种子程序生成多语言可执行代码池，利用执行结果标注翻译候选对，训练奖励模型并采用GRPO优化基础LLM，覆盖25种语言的600个有向语言对。同时引入HumanEval-X++基准评估多语言翻译能力。在Qwen-3.5 4B和9B模型上，平均提升13%，中端语言提升21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经代码翻译主要集中于少数流行语言（如C++、Java、Python），而现实需求涉及多语言间的翻译（many-to-many），且平行语料稀疏，导致翻译结果虽语法合理但无法执行。因此需要一种可靠的、可扩展的方法来提升多语言代码翻译的执行正确性。

Method: 1) 数据生成：从可验证的Python种子程序出发，通过多语言执行验证扩展为可执行代码池。2) 偏好标注：利用基础LLM在语言对间生成翻译候选，根据执行结果（成功/失败）标注偏好。3) 奖励模型训练：基于偏好数据训练奖励模型，用于评估跨语言翻译质量。4) 模型优化：使用GRPO（组相对策略优化）在600个有向语言对（25×24）上优化基础LLM，以奖励模型作为信号。

Result: 在HumanEval-X++和现有基准上，该方法在未训练基线基础上取得一致提升。4B模型在HumanEval-X++上平均提升13%，其中中端语言提升21%。验证了数据生成、训练和基准评估的整套流程的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一种可靠的多语言代码翻译方法，通过执行监督的偏好强化学习有效提升了翻译的执行正确性，为后续进一步提升多语言翻译质量奠定了基础。

Abstract: Code translation must preserve executable behavior across many programming languages, yet neural code translation has largely focused on a few popular languages such as C++, Java, and Python. This leaves a niche, many-to-many setting where parallel supervision is sparse, producing plausible but non-executable translations. We address this setting with preference-based reinforcement learning driven by execution-based supervision. Our pipeline firstly expands verifiable seed Python programs into a multilingual pool of execution-validated codes. Using the pool, a base LLM generates translation candidates across language pairs, which we label by their execution outcomes. The resulting preferences are used to train a reward model that scores cross-language translation quality. Finally, we optimize our base LLMs with GRPO over 600 directed language pairs (25 x 24) using the reward model as a signal. To evaluate the niche translation capability, we introduce HumanEval-X++, an execution-based benchmark that extends HumanEval-X to a broad many-to-many language space. We evaluate our approach using Qwen-3.5 4B and 9B models. On HumanEval-X++ and existing benchmarks, it yields consistent gains over the untrained baselines. In particular, the 4B model achieves an average improvement of 13% across all languages on HumanEval-X++, with a gain of 21% on mid-tier languages. Our study establishes a reliable approach of data generation, training, and benchmarking, paving the way toward further bootstrapping the quality of many-to-many translation for programming languages.

</details>


### [85] [Scaling Creative Writing Beyond Story-Centric Data with Attribute-Guided Genre Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13947)
*Hwan Chang,Yongil Kim,Heuiyeen Yeen,Yireun Kim,Jinsik Lee,Hwanhee Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种属性引导的体裁扩展框架，用于将大语言模型的创意写作数据从故事主导扩展到多种创意体裁。通过分离主题广度与体裁形式控制，利用人类作者的故事提示作为创意种子，结合人工整理的体裁属性约束结构、风格和格式规范，生成高质量的体裁忠实指令-响应对，并构建了包含50K示例、覆盖13种创意体裁的Multi-Genre Collection。实验表明，在该数据上微调的模型在分布外写作基准和保留体裁诊断上持续超越基线和现有写作语料库训练的模型，且控制下的体裁扩展（而非仅故事中心扩展）是提升创意写作能力的关键驱动因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高质量创意写作数据以故事为中心，限制了语言模型遵循多样化创意格式的结构和功能惯例的能力，亟需一种能系统扩展创意写作数据体裁多样性的方法。

Method: 提出属性引导的体裁扩展框架：将主题广度与体裁形式控制分离，使用人类作者的故事提示作为多样化创意种子，结合人工整理的体裁属性（结构、风格、格式规范）来约束生成，通过强LLM生成体裁忠实的指令-响应对，并进行质量过滤，最终构建多体裁语料库。

Result: 构建了Multi-Genre Collection（50K示例，13种体裁），包括故事、说唱、歌词、剧本、游戏设计、角色设计等。微调模型在分布外写作基准和保留体裁诊断上持续超越基线和现有写作语料库训练的模型。体裁数量消融实验表明，受控的体裁扩展是提升创意写作能力的关键。

Conclusion: 受控的体裁扩展（而非仅故事中心的数据扩展）是驱动鲁棒创意写作能力的关键因素，所提框架能有效弥补现有创意写作数据在体裁多样性上的不足。

Abstract: High-quality creative writing data for large language models (LLMs) remains dominated by story-centric data, limiting models' ability to follow the structural and functional conventions of diverse creative formats. We propose an attribute-guided genre expansion framework for scaling creative writing data beyond story generation. By separating thematic breadth from genre-form control, our framework leverages human-authored story prompts as diverse creative seeds, while utilizing manually curated genre attributes to enforce distinct structural, stylistic, and formatting conventions. We combine these to prompt strong LLMs for genre-faithful query-response pairs, which are then quality-filtered. Applying this framework, we construct the Multi-Genre Collection, a 50K-example corpus spanning 13 creative genres, including story, rap, lyrics, scripts, game design, character design, and other creative formats. Experiments across out-of-distribution writing benchmarks and held-out genre diagnostics demonstrate that models fine-tuned on our data consistently surpass not only base models and writing-specialized baselines, but also models trained on existing writing corpora. Genre-count ablations further indicate that controlled genre expansion, rather than story-centric scaling alone, is a key driver of robust creative writing capability.

</details>


### [86] [Repair, Not Improvement: Decomposing Constrained Decoding in Tool-Call Abstention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13959)
*Janghoon Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究在函数调用场景下，语法约束解码（grammar-constrained decoding）对模型“工具放弃”（tool abstention）行为的影响。通过设计三种条件（使用完全相同的提示词），将语法规则的两种功能分离：决定生成何时停止（停止符）与决定哪些词元可被发射（枚举）。在0.6B至4B的开源模型上，使用配对英文和韩文测试项进行语言对比。结果发现：相较于无约束解码，先前的约束解码方法在6个实验单元中有4个对放弃率产生显著负面影响（最差-29.5个百分点），无显著正面影响；分解后，对于最小模型（韩文任务），停止符约束造成-20.0的损失，枚举约束带来+19.5的提升，两者合计仅-0.5。该干预主要恢复了形式（698例修复的放弃中545例原本无有效答案，0例为评分者拒绝的判断）。在需要工具的任务上效果始终为正。两个预先注册的语言假设均未得到支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于约束生成的研究认为解码器对格式约束的贡献很小，但在第7节警告不要将该结论外推到约束编码了正确性要求的情况，并点名函数调用。工具放弃正是这种情形的极端体现：枚举约束会限制答案集合，而“不调用任何工具”这一选项可能首当其冲被丢弃。现有文献排除了这种情形，本文旨在测量这一被排除的案例，并分解语法约束在工具放弃上的两种作用。

Method: 设计三种条件（均使用同一字节完全相同的提示词）来分离语法的两种功能：停止符（决定生成何时停止）和枚举（决定哪些词元可被发射）。在0.6B至4B参数的开源模型上，使用配对英文和韩文测试项进行评测，使得语言比较在项目内进行（within-item）。以无约束解码为基线，比较不同约束条件下的工具放弃率。

Result: 相对于无约束解码，先前约束解码方法在6个实验单元中有4个对放弃率产生显著负效应（区间排除零），最差达-29.5个百分点，无显著正效应。分解效应：以最小模型（韩文）为例，停止符代价为-20.0，枚举收益为+19.5，两者合计-0.5。在698例被修复的放弃中，545例原本无有效答案，0例是评分者拒绝的判断。在需要工具的任务上，效应始终为正。两个预先注册的语言假设均不成立。

Conclusion: 语法约束解码对工具放弃的影响是混合的：停止符约束可能损害放弃率（即强制生成导致模型不应调用工具时也调用），而枚举约束可能提升放弃率（限制合法词元使模型更可能选择不调用）。净效应较小，且干预主要恢复了形式（将空答案转为工具调用）而非提升了正确性。由于聚合数据对干预更友好，将放弃率作为主要指标反而更不利。语言对比的假设未获支持。

Abstract: Function calling is what the recent accounting of constrained generation explicitly sets aside: it finds the decoder's contribution small for format constraints, then warns in its Section 7 against extrapolating where a constraint encodes a correctness requirement, and names function calling as one. Tool abstention is that case at its sharpest: an enum leaves the wording of an answer alone and narrows the set of answers there are, and declining to call anything is the first it drops. We measure the excluded case. Three conditions over one byte-identical prompt separate a grammar's two jobs: it fixes where generation stops as well as which tokens may be emitted. We evaluate open-weight models from 0.6B to 4B on matched English and Korean items, so the language comparison is made within item. Against an unconstrained decoder, prior work's contrast is negative on abstention in four of six cells with intervals excluding zero, worst -29.5 points, and positive with an interval excluding zero in none. The total is a sum with opposite signs: on the smallest model in Korean the stop token costs -20.0, the enum returns +19.5, and the two leave -0.5. What it recovers is form: of 698 abstentions repaired, 545 had no readable answer and 0 were judgements the scorer refused. On tool-needed items it is positive throughout; abstention leads because it is the preregistered measure, and the pooled number being kinder to the intervention makes moving to it worse rather than better. Both preregistered language claims fail.

</details>


### [87] [Batch-wise Adaptive Pruning: Periodic Neuron Activation-Aware Weight Pruning for Language Reasoning Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14003)
*Yongmin Kim,Shota Takashiro,Yusuke Iwasawa,Takeshi Kojima,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种针对大型推理模型（LRM）批处理推理的免训练自适应剪枝方法，利用周期性 top-k 选择与激活记忆机制，解决了因激活分布偏移导致的精度崩塌问题，在保持推理加速的同时显著提升了批处理场景下的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的免训练自适应剪枝方法在批处理推理中严重退化：由于批处理共享单一剪枝掩码，激活值跨样本聚合后分布改变，离线校准的阈值不再匹配，导致实际稀疏度偏离目标、推理任务准确率崩溃。需要一种专为批处理设计、无需重新训练的自适应剪枝方法。

Method: 方法包含两个组件：1) 用周期性的 top-k 选择替代阈值选择，基于聚合后的重要性分数直接选取 top-k 神经元，不受激活分布偏移影响，且每个更新周期只执行一次选择而非每步都做，保持加速效果；2) 引入激活记忆（activation memory），在更新阶段间累积重要性分数，使长期推理中周期性复现的重要神经元得以保留。

Result: 在多种推理基准上，以 DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B 模型、batch size 4、目标稀疏度 50% 的条件测试，所提方法相比此前最优的自适应剪枝方法平均准确率提升 39.7 个百分点，并在实际稀疏度 50% 时达到相对于密集推理的 1.40 倍加速。

Conclusion: 该方法在批处理推理场景下有效解决了现有自适应剪枝方法的分布偏移问题，实现了高准确率与显著加速，且无需额外训练，适用于大型推理模型的生产级部署。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) achieve strong performance on complex tasks through extended chain-of-thought generation, but incur substantial computational costs during inference. In production settings, batched inference is essential for high throughput, yet the existing training-free adaptive pruning methods we evaluate severely degrade in this regime. Because a batch must share a single pruning mask, these methods aggregate activations across samples and then apply threshold-based selection; the threshold, calibrated offline on unaggregated activations, no longer matches the aggregated distribution, so the realized sparsity ratio drifts and accuracy on reasoning tasks collapses under batched inference.
  In this work, we propose a training-free adaptive pruning method designed specifically for batched inference in LRMs, built on two components. First, we replace threshold-based selection with periodic top-k selection over the aggregated importance scores, which is unaffected by the shift that aggregation induces in the activation distribution, and which runs selection once per update period rather than at every token, preserving the speedup. Second, based on the observation that important neurons re-fire periodically during long reasoning generation, we introduce an activation memory that accumulates importance across update phases so that recurring neurons are retained.
  Experiments on diverse reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that our method outperforms the previous state-of-the-art adaptive pruning method by 39.7 percentage points in average accuracy at batch size 4 with 50% target sparsity on DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B, and reaches 1.40x speedup over dense inference at 50% actual sparsity.

</details>


### [88] [S2Dialog: Multimodal Dialogue Retrieval with Semantic and Acoustic-Style Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14029)
*Xueqi Wang,Zhigang Wang,Runqing Zhang,Zhenqi Jia,Junfeng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出S2Dialog，一个用于多模态对话级别语义-风格检索的统一框架。它包含对话级文本检索器、对话级声学检索器以及对话级文本-声学对比学习，在DailyTalk数据集上取得了优秀检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索方法多局限于话语级别或单模态匹配，难以捕捉整个对话的全局语义一致性和风格一致性。多模态对话检索需要同时考虑文本语义和声学会话风格，但缺乏有效的对话级别检索方法。

Method: S2Dialog包含三个核心组件：1）对话级文本检索器，将对话文本编码为对话级表示；2）对话级声学检索器，将对话声学特征编码为对话级表示；3）对话级文本-声学对比学习，对齐语义和风格相似的对话，同时区分不相关的对话。

Result: 在多模态对话数据集DailyTalk上的大量实验表明，S2Dialog取得了优秀的检索性能。

Conclusion: S2Dialog通过对话级别的文本和声学检索以及对比学习，有效实现了多模态对话的语义-风格检索，填补了现有方法在对话级别多模态检索方面的空白。

Abstract: Multimodal dialogue retrieval aims to retrieve dialogues from multimodal dialogue banks that are similar to a target dialogue in terms of both textual semantics and acoustic conversational styles. Such dialogue-level retrieval is crucial for many dialogue-related tasks, including Emotion Recognition in Conversation, Spoken Dialogue Systems, and Conversational Speech Synthesis, where external dialogue examples can provide valuable semantic and stylistic references. However, existing retrieval methods are still largely limited to utterance-level or unimodal matching, and often fail to capture the global semantic coherence and stylistic consistency of an entire dialogue. To address this gap, we propose S2Dialog, a unified framework for dialogue-level semantic-style retrieval from multimodal dialogue banks. Specifically, S2Dialog consists of a Dialogue-level Textual Retriever and a Dialogue-level Acoustic Retriever, which encode the textual and acoustic modalities of a dialogue into dialogue-level representations, respectively. To further enhance multimodal retrieval, we introduce Dialogue-level Textual-Acoustic Contrastive Learning, which aligns semantically and stylistically similar dialogues while distinguishing unrelated ones. Extensive experiments on the multimodal dialogue dataset DailyTalk demonstrate that S2Dialog achieves outstanding retrieval performance.

</details>


### [89] [HERMES: a multi-agent framework for structured knowledge extraction from ultra-long documents in geoscience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14055)
*Ziqi Song,Zongyuan Xiang,James G. Ogg,Bruce S. Lieberman,Gabi Ogg,Natalia López Carranza,Wen Du,Yufei Ye,Shuan Li,Zhong Peng,Shaoqi Yu,Juye Wei,Ying Zhou,Jieping Ye,Jiang Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出HERMES多智能体框架，从超长科学文档（如55卷古生物学专著）中提取结构化数据，构建了含32,277个化石分类实体和451,878个属性的数据库，F1约0.90/0.91，效率提升约6倍，可迁移至其他地球科学领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球科学领域的权威知识仍困于非结构化的历史文献和专著中，复杂排版和自由文本阻碍了计算访问，亟需将这类文献转化为符合FAIR原则的结构化数据。

Method: 提出HERMES——一种可扩展的多智能体框架，通过协调大语言模型，集成领域约束、验证规则和证据追溯，在统一文档级抽取流程中处理文本、表格、图表和标题，实现超长科学文档的结构化数据提取。

Result: 在55卷《无脊椎古生物学论丛》上应用，生成包含32,277个化石分类实体和451,878个属性的结构化数据库；实体抽取F1约0.90，属性抽取F1约0.91；每卷效率相比纯人工基线提升约6倍；在古地磁学和地球化学上的零训练迁移测试表现良好。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一条将历史科学文献转化为FAIR导向结构化数据的实用路径，为数据密集型学科和大规模知识集成提供了可持续基础设施。

Abstract: Authoritative scientific knowledge in geoscience remains largely trapped in legacy monographs and historical literature, where unstructured text and complex layouts hinder computational access. We introduce HERMES, a scalable multi-agent framework that extracts structured data from ultra-long scientific documents. Using a coordinating large language model, HERMES integrates domain constraints, validation rules and evidence tracing within a unified document-level extraction process that incorporates parsed text, tables, figures and captions. Applied to the 55-volume Treatise on Invertebrate Paleontology, the system produced a structured database of 32,277 fossil taxonomic entities and 451,878 attributes, released online at https://treatise.geolex.org. Extraction performance remained stable across fossil groups (average F1 scores of approximately 0.90 for entities and 0.91 for attributes), improving per-volume efficiency approximately sixfold relative to the tested fully manual baseline. Evaluation in palaeomagnetism and geochemistry, conducted without additional model training, demonstrated transfer across distinct geoscience domains. This work provides a practical pathway to transform historical scientific literature into FAIR-oriented structured data, offering a sustainable infrastructure for data-intensive disciplines and large-scale knowledge integration.

</details>


### [90] [The conditional superiority of fast silicon sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14079)
*Nickolas Hock Yuen Lam,Ji Xuan Voo,Xiangyu Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了"硅采样"（使用前沿AI模型生成合成调查数据）中快速与慢速模式的算法保真度。研究发现，当前硅采样方法仍处于早期开发阶段，需谨慎使用：虽能适度估计人口均值，但低估了意见方差并扭曲了潜在语境空间。值得注意的是，快速模式在计算效率和保真度上均优于传统慢速模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨快速硅采样是否会降低算法保真度，以及比较快速与慢速模式在人口估计中的表现，旨在评估硅采样作为一种高效调查方法的可靠性和有效性。

Method: 在新加坡全国代表性调查样本中，扩展并评估了已有硅采样工作，通过对比"快速"和"慢速"两种硅采样模式的算法保真度来进行研究。

Result: 硅采样能产生适度忠实的人口均值估计，但持续低估意见方差并扭曲人类意见背后的潜在语境空间。快速硅采样在计算资源和运行时间上显著更高效，且在算法保真度上单调优于慢速模式。

Conclusion: 当代前沿模型的硅采样仍处于早期开发阶段，需极其谨慎使用。尽管存在局限性，快速硅采样模式相对优于传统慢速模式，为未来优化提供了方向。

Abstract: Silicon sampling can produce surprisingly good population estimates at times. Does doing it fast attenuate such fidelity? In this study, we extend and assess ongoing work in silicon sampling by comparing the algorithmic fidelity of "fast" and "slow" modes of silicon sampling among a nationally representative sample of Singaporean survey respondents. We find that silicon sampling with contemporary frontier models remains a method in early development to be used only with great caution. While silicon samples are able to produce moderately faithful estimates of population means, they continue to understate opinion variance and distort the latent contextual space behind human opinions. Conditional on such limitations, we find "fast" modes of silicon sampling to be relatively superior to traditional "slow" modes of silicon sampling. Fast silicon sampling is significantly more efficient in compute resources and run-time while being monotonically superior to slower modes of sampling in algorithmic fidelity.

</details>


### [91] [Leading-Silence Augmentation and Multi-Stage Synthetic Supervision for the Second MLC-SLM Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14150)
*Kexin Shi,Renhe Sun,Yuge Huang,Ximeng Wang,Jiayi Zhou,Jian Liu,Malu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对第二届多语言会话语音语言模型挑战赛的两个任务（说话人日志与识别、会话语音理解）提出了改进方案。任务1通过随机前导静音裁剪、一致时间戳校正和指数移动平均训练策略微调VibeVoice-ASR-7B，将tcpMER从18.30%降至16.73%；任务2通过多模态候选生成、静音音频过滤和分布匹配增强构建合成问答对，微调Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct进行标记直接回答，将准确率从83.0%提升至86.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言会话场景下缺乏标准化的说话人日志与识别基准，且会话语音理解任务不提供问答训练集，需要开发无需语句边界和说话人标签先验信息的端到端方法。

Method: 任务1：微调VibeVoice-ASR-7B，采用随机前导静音裁剪（减少冗余静音干扰）、一致时间戳校正（对齐预测与标注时间戳）和指数移动平均训练策略（平滑模型参数）。任务2：通过多模态候选生成（利用视觉/音频线索生成候选问答）、静音音频过滤（去除无效片段）和分布匹配增强（对齐合成数据与真实分布）构建合成问答对，微调Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct进行标记直接回答（在输出中添加标签指示回答来源）。

Result: 任务1：裁剪使tcpMER从18.30%降至17.27%，EMA进一步降至16.73%。任务2：联合应用分布匹配增强和标记直接回答将准确率从83.0%提升至86.0%。

Conclusion: 所提出的数据增强和训练策略能有效提升无需先验分割信息的会话语音处理性能，表明合成数据生成与参数平滑在弱监督场景下的价值。

Abstract: The second Multilingual Conversational Speech Language Model (MLC-SLM) Challenge evaluates two tasks over complete, unsegmented multilingual conversations: speaker diarization and recognition (Task 1) and conversational speech understanding (Task 2). Neither task provides oracle utterance boundaries or speaker labels at evaluation, and Task 2 provides no question-answer training set. For Task 1, we fine-tune VibeVoice-ASR-7B with random leading-silence cropping, consistent timestamp correction, and an exponential moving average (EMA) training strategy. For Task 2, we construct synthetic question-answer pairs through multimodal candidate generation, silent-audio filtering, and distribution-matched augmentation, and fine-tune Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct for tagged direct answering. On the Task 1 evaluation set, cropping reduces tcpMER from 18.30% to 17.27%, and EMA further reduces it to 16.73%. On the Task 2 evaluation set, jointly applying distribution-matched augmentation and tagged direct answering raises accuracy from 83.0% to 86.0%.

</details>


### [92] [How Much Do Legal RAG Systems Still Hallucinate?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14210)
*Souvick Das,Sallam Abualhaija,Domenico Bianculli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对法律领域检索增强生成（RAG）系统中的幻觉问题进行了细粒度分析，评估了八个法律RAG系统在GDPR（英文）和法国民法典（法文）两个语料上的表现，发现幻觉依然普遍存在（最差系统近半数回答存在幻觉），且虚假前提问题（需拒绝的错误假设）导致高幻觉率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律领域的RAG系统存在严重的幻觉问题，不基于事实的回答可能带来严重后果，但此前缺乏对该问题的细粒度理解和系统性评估。

Method: 对八个法律RAG系统在两个法律语料库（英文GDPR和法国民法典）上进行细粒度分析，采用声明级和回答级评估，报告幻觉密度和严重程度，按问题类别和用户角色分析性能，并在142个法律专家编写的问题上进行验证。

Result: 幻觉问题仍然普遍存在：最佳系统的回答中不足10%包含幻觉，而最差系统近半回答存在幻觉。虚假前提问题（包含需拒绝的错误假设）在人工编写的问题中导致高幻觉率。

Conclusion: 法律RAG系统中的幻觉问题严峻，尤其是对虚假前提问题的处理能力不足，需要进一步改进以提升法律AI的可靠性。

Abstract: Hallucination is a major challenge for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems in the legal domain, where ungrounded answers can lead to serious consequences. To better understand this problem, we conduct a fine-grained analysis of hallucination behavior in eight legal RAG systems across two legal corpora, the GDPR (in English) and a national civil law (in French). Using claim-level and answer-level evaluation, we report on hallucination density and severity, analyze performance across question categories and user personas, and validate our findings on an independent set of 142 legal-expert-authored questions. Our results show that hallucinations remain pervasive, ranging from less than 10% of responses for the best-performing systems to nearly half in the worst case. We further find that false-premise questions, containing incorrect assumptions that must be rejected, produce high hallucination rates on the manually-drafted questions.

</details>


### [93] [The More Popular, The Harder to Forget: Adaptive Popularity for LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14229)
*Anna Borisiuk,Andrey Savchenko,Alexander Panchenko,Elena Tutubalina*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出AdaPop方法，通过自适应流行度调节（结合token置信度与事实流行度指数）和双上升控制器自动平衡遗忘-保留损失，使LLM遗忘任务在保留罕见事实的同时更彻底地遗忘流行事实，泄露量比现有方法减少约5倍（释义查询）和1.6倍（对抗改写）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM遗忘方法对所有训练样本施加统一的梯度压力，忽略了训练数据中事实的流行度差异：流行事实在预训练中被记忆得更深、更难以移除，而罕见事实更容易被遗忘。这种统一处理导致遗忘不彻底（流行事实残留）或过度遗忘（罕见事实被误删）。

Method: AdaPop方法包含两个核心组件：(1) 自适应流行度调节——利用局部token置信度反映模型对当前事实的掌握程度，并结合外部代理（如Wikidata页面链接数、LLM-as-Judge评分）为每个事实计算一个流行度相关的指数，动态调整该事实在遗忘损失中的权重；(2) 双上升控制器（dual-ascent controller）——自动调节每一轮的保留惩罚强度，在遗忘目标与保留目标之间实现自适应平衡，避免手动调参。

Result: 在三个模型族和两个基准测试上，AdaPop在释义查询下泄露的已遗忘内容比现有方法少约5倍，在对抗性改写下少约1.6倍。内部指标分析显示：遗忘集的隐藏状态比现有方法更远离遗忘前模型的隐藏状态，而保留集的表示保持稳定（与遗忘前模型接近）。

Conclusion: 流行度自适应调节是提升LLM遗忘效果的关键设计，能够更精准地区分流行事实与罕见事实的遗忘需求，在减少泄露的同时保持模型对保留知识的完整性。双上升控制器自动平衡遗忘-保留损失，避免了繁琐的手动调参。

Abstract: Popular facts are memorised more deeply during pretraining and resist removal longer than rare ones, yet existing LLM unlearning methods apply uniform gradient pressure regardless of training-data frequency. We propose the AdaPop (Adaptive Popularity) method, which combines local token confidence with a per-fact popularity-dependent exponent derived from an external proxy (e.g., Wikidata sitelinks, LLM-as-Judge), and automates the forget-retain balance via a dual-ascent controller that adjusts the retain penalty each epoch. Across three model families and two benchmarks, AdaPop leaks ~5x less forgotten content than competing methods under paraphrased queries and ~1.6x less under adversarial reformulations. We support our analysis with internal metrics: under our method, forget-set hidden states move further from the pre-unlearning model's states than under other methods, while retain-set representations remain close.

</details>


### [94] [SimpleOPD: Simple Tokenizer-Agnostic On-Policy Distillation for Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14277)
*Haonan He,Haodi Lei,Yun Luo,Haoran Zhang,Shunkai Zhang,Yizhuo Li,Shengji Tang,Zhilin Wang,Runzhe Zhan,Lei Bai,Ganqu Cui,Fangchen Yu,Yafu Li,Peng Ye,Ning Ding,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究将长上下文推理教师模型（如SU-01）的证明推理能力通过在线策略蒸馏（OPD）迁移至短上下文学生模型。针对分词器不匹配、师生分布差异、响应长度爆炸和训练不稳定等挑战，提出在共享文本空间中对齐相同文本片段的令牌，并引入学生参考KL损失以及掩码特殊终止令牌（如<|im_start|>和<|im_end|>）的优势，从而约束学生偏离初始策略，缓解分布差异并促进长度稳定增长。在多种学生模型（Qwen3、Qwen3.5、Intern-S2、GLM-4.7、Gemma-4）上实验表明，数学推理尤其是自然语言数学证明能力持续提升，其中Intern-S2-Preview在ProofBench上提升21.2分达到55.2，超越Gemini-2.5-Pro，并在HLE、HiPhO等科学基准上也取得改进，表明OPD迁移的推理能力可泛化至数学领域之外。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）有望将强教师模型的推理能力迁移至学生模型，但将长上下文推理教师应用于短上下文学生时面临实际挑战：分词器不匹配（教师与学生可能使用不同分词器）、师生分布差异（教师生成的响应长度和风格与学生不同）、响应长度爆炸（学生可能生成过长响应导致截断）以及训练不稳定。现有方法难以直接处理这些障碍，因此需要针对性的技术来有效实现跨架构、跨上下文长度的推理能力迁移。

Method: 1）在共享文本空间中进行OPD，对齐学生和教师分词器下占据相同文本片段的令牌，解决分词器不匹配问题。2）引入学生参考KL损失，约束学生策略不偏离其初始策略过远，缓解师生分布差异。3）掩码特殊终止令牌（如<|im_start|>和<|im_end|>）的优势，防止学生过早终止生成，从而避免响应长度爆炸和频繁截断，促进生成长度稳定增长。通过这些策略，实现从长上下文推理模型（SU-01）向短上下文学生模型的有效知识迁移。

Result: 在同类（同系列）和异类（不同系列）学生模型上均取得一致改进：Qwen3、Qwen3.5、Intern-S2、GLM-4.7、Gemma-4在数学推理尤其是自然语言数学证明上表现提升。Intern-S2-Preview在ProofBench上提升21.2分，达到55.2，超越Gemini-2.5-Pro。此外，在HLE（硬语言理解）和HiPhO（物理）等科学基准上也观察到改进，表明OPD迁移的推理能力可泛化至数学训练领域之外。

Conclusion: 本文提出的面向长上下文教师与短上下文学生的在线策略蒸馏方法有效解决了分词器不匹配、分布差异、长度爆炸和训练不稳定等挑战。通过共享文本空间令牌对齐、学生参考KL损失和终止令牌掩码，实现了从强推理模型到小型模型的成功知识迁移，显著提升了数学证明推理能力，并展现出跨领域的泛化潜力。该工作为利用长上下文推理模型增强短上下文学生模型提供了一种实用且有效的蒸馏框架。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) offers a promising way to transfer reasoning capabilities from stronger teacher models, but applying it to long-context reasoning teachers and short-context students introduces practical challenges, including tokenizer mismatch, teacher-student distribution mismatch, response length explosion, and training instability. In this work, we study this setting by transferring proof-reasoning capabilities from the long-context reasoning model SU-01 to short-context student models. To handle tokenizer differences, we perform OPD in a shared text space and align only tokens that occupy identical text spans under the student and teacher tokenizers. To mitigate the problem of excessive generation length and frequent truncation, we introduce a student reference KL loss and mask the advantages of special termination tokens such as </think> and <|im_end|>. This strategy constrains the student from drifting excessively from its initial policy, thereby mitigating the teacher-student distribution mismatch problem and fostering steady length growth. Experiments on both same-family and different-family student models, including Qwen3, Qwen3.5, Intern-S2, GLM-4.7, Gemma-4, show consistent gains in mathematical reasoning, especially natural-language math proving. Notably, Intern-S2-Preview improves by 21.2 points on ProofBench, reaching 55.2 and surpassing Gemini-2.5-Pro. It also improves on science benchmarks such as HLE and HiPhO, suggesting that OPD transfers reasoning capabilities that generalize beyond the mathematical training domain.

</details>


### [95] [Envs-FORGE: Frontier-Optimized Reward-Grounded Environment Synthesis for Agent RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14312)
*Xiaojun Wu,Cehao Yang,Honghao Liu,Xueyuan Lin,Zhichao Shi,Hao Zhou,Xuhui Jiang,Chengjin Xu,Jia Li,Jian Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Envs-FORGE 是一种针对终端智能体强化学习的自适应环境合成策略，通过将验证器奖励转换为每种子样本的环境生成动作，利用混合整数线性规划选择最优动作，动态调整任务难度，在多个基准上显著提升了 Pass@1 指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有固定指令策略（如 Few-shot、Self-Instruct、Evol-Instruct）对所有种子样本使用相同的提示策略，无法根据当前策略的需求动态调整任务难度（更简单、更难或不同），导致训练环境缺乏可执行性和合适的难度。

Method: Envs-FORGE 首先估计每个种子样本的通过率，然后围绕目标学习前沿计算六个投影方向动作，并通过求解每个样本的混合整数线性规划（MILP）来选择最优生成动作。所选动作驱动指令、固件、参考解、测试用例和 Docker 环境的同步重写，只有通过验证的环境才进入 RL 训练。

Result: 在 Qwen 3.5 35B 上，Envs-FORGE 在 tb-core 上将 Pass@1 从 40.0% 提升至 49.2%（+9.2），在 tb-2.0 上从 23.0% 提升至 29.4%（+6.4），超过最强固定策略基线 2.4 和 2.1 个百分点；在 SWE-bench Verified 上达到 77.1%（Base 为 73.4%）；在 4B-35B 模型上均提升 6.8-9.2 个百分点。

Conclusion: Envs-FORGE 通过自适应环境生成策略有效提升了 RL 终端智能体的训练效果，且不增加合成 token 开销（2.27M-2.88M），代码已开源。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for terminal agents needs executable training environments with reliable rewards and useful difficulty. Fixed recipes such as few-shot, Self-Instruct, and Evol-Instruct apply the same prompting policy to every seed, even when the current policy would benefit from a harder, easier, or simply different task. We present Envs-FORGE, a prompting policy that converts verifier rewards into per-seed environment-synthesis actions. Envs-FORGE estimates seed pass rates, scores six projection--direction actions around a target learning frontier, and solves a per-seed mixed-integer linear program (MILP) to choose the action that conditions generation. The selected action drives synchronized rewriting of the instruction, fixtures, oracle solution, tests, and Docker environment; only gold-verified bundles enter RL training. The indexed MILP form also supports optional soft skill coverage for portfolio planning. On Qwen 3.5 35B, Envs-FORGE improves Pass@1 over Base by 9.2 percentage points on tb-core (40.0% to 49.2%) and 6.4 points on tb-2.0 (23.0% to 29.4%), exceeding the strongest fixed-recipe baseline by 2.4 and 2.1 points. It reaches 77.1% on SWE-bench Verified versus 73.4% for Base, and improves tb-core by 6.8--9.2 points across the evaluated 4B--35B models. All synthesis methods export 100 verified environments and use 2.27M--2.88M synthesis tokens, placing the comparison at the same downstream training-set size and the same operational scale. The source code is available at https://github.com/DataArcTech/DataArc-SynData-Toolkit/.

</details>


### [96] [Local and Global Regimes of Geometric Complexity in Language Model Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14361)
*Arwa Osman,Marco Baroni,Iuri Macocco*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究词汇多样性（数据集中唯一最后一个token的数量）如何影响语言模型表示的内在维度（ID）估计。发现一个尺度依赖的转变：低词汇多样性时，较少唯一词导致更高ID；高词汇多样性时，较多唯一词导致更高ID。作者推导出一个无参数的精确公式预测转变点，并在所有尺度上匹配。结果提醒解释ID需谨慎，同时两个ID regimes揭示了LLM中语言数据组织的一般原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内在维度（ID）被广泛用于探测语言模型的表示复杂度，但尚不清楚ID差异反映的是语言本身的属性还是数据集构建方式的人为因素。本文旨在厘清词汇多样性（唯一最后一个token的数量）如何影响ID估计，并探究其背后的机制。

Method: 通过系统性地改变数据集的词汇多样性（即唯一最后一个token的数量），在不同尺度下测量内在维度（ID），观察ID随词汇多样性的变化规律，并推导出一个无参数的精确公式来预测两个ID regimes之间的转变点。

Result: 发现了尺度依赖的转变：低词汇多样性时，较少唯一词产生更高ID；高词汇多样性时，较多唯一词产生更高ID。推导的转变点公式在所有测试尺度上与实验观察一致。

Conclusion: 一方面，解释一组表示的内在维度作为其复杂度的直接线索时需要谨慎；另一方面，两个ID regimes的发现揭示了LLM中语言数据组织的一般原则，为理解其内部流形结构提供了新视角。

Abstract: Intrinsic dimensionality (ID) is widely used to probe the representational complexity of language models, but it remains unclear whether ID differences reflect properties of language itself or artefacts of how the underlying dataset was constructed. In this paper, we focus specifically on how lexical diversity, the number of unique last-token items present in a dataset, affects ID estimates of that dataset. We find a scale-dependent transition between two regimes: at low lexical diversity, conditions with fewer unique final words produce higher ID, while at high lexical diversity, this ordering reverses, and conditions with more unique words produce higher ID. We derive an exact, parameter-free formula for the point at which this reversal occurs, which matches the observed transition point at every scale tested. On the one hand, our results highlight how care must be taken when interpreting the intrinsic dimensionality of a set of representations as a straightforward cue of their complexity. On the other hand, our discovery of the two ID regimes reveals a general principle of organisation of linguistic data in LLMs that sheds new light on their inner manifold structures.

</details>


### [97] [A Survey of Large Models in Sports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14377)
*Yichen Xu,Jianzhe Ma,Chuhan Wang,Zhonghao Cao,Liangyu Chen,Wenxuan Wang,Qin Jin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: This paper provides a comprehensive survey of large models (particularly multimodal large language models) applied to the sports domain, covering tasks and applications across different participant groups, sports-related datasets and benchmarks, and a critical discussion of current challenges and future directions, with the goal of establishing a foundation for large-model-driven sports intelligence.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sports have growing global importance for health, culture, social connection, and economic growth. The rapid advancement of large models, especially (M)LLMs, offers transformative potential to reshape how sports are understood, analyzed, and interacted with, but there lacks a systematic survey of this emerging intersection.

Method: The paper conducts a comprehensive survey organizing research across three dimensions: (i) overview of tasks and applications tailored to different participant groups (e.g., athletes, coaches, fans, administrators); (ii) detailed analysis of sports-related datasets and benchmarks used to train and evaluate large models; (iii) critical discussion of current challenges and future research directions.

Result: The survey provides a structured taxonomy of large-model applications in sports, an extensive catalog of sports datasets and benchmarks, and identifies key challenges such as data scarcity, domain-specific fine-tuning, real-time processing, multimodal integration, and ethical considerations.

Conclusion: This survey aims to establish a foundation for advancing research and practical development of large-model-driven sports intelligence, serving as a reference for future work and offering an open-source GitHub repository with curated resources.

Abstract: Sports have witnessed growing global enthusiasm in recent years, serving as a vital force for physical health, cultural exchange, social connection, and economic growth. The rapid advancement of large models, particularly (multimodal) large language models (M)LLMs, has demonstrated transformative potential to reshape sports understanding, analysis, and interaction across diverse domains. This paper presents a comprehensive survey of large models in sports, including (i) an overview of tasks and applications across different participant groups; (ii) a detailed analysis of sports-related datasets and benchmarks; and (iii) a critical discussion of current challenges and future directions. Our goal is to establish a foundation for advancing research and practical development of large-model-driven sports intelligence. An open-source GitHub repository is maintained at: https://github.com/Road2Redemption/Awesome_Large_Models_In_Sports1.

</details>


### [98] [Information Satisfaction: A Reader-Centered Axis for Summarization Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14457)
*Isabel Cachola,William Walden,Reno Kriz,Mark Dredze*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文聚焦摘要评估中一个被忽视的问题：现有指标（如ROUGE、BERTScore、LLM评判）无法衡量摘要对个体用户的信息效用，而用户的角色和专业知识背景（persona）是比查询更稳定的信号。研究发现，即使强大的LLM评估指标也无法通过基础的信息内容扰动测试，且与传统及基于LLM的指标一样与人类专家对信息满意度的判断一致性差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前摘要评估指标主要关注通用质量（如ROUGE、BERTScore）或特定属性（可读性、事实性），但无法衡量摘要对特定用户的实用价值。不同背景的用户（如生物医学研究员与家庭医生）信息需求不同，而用户通常不会在查询中完整表述需求；相比之下，用户的角色和专业知识（persona）相对稳定，可作为评估摘要信息满意度的实用信号。

Method: 通过信息内容和persona差异的扰动测试检验现有指标的敏感性；同时开展专家人工评估，基于特定用户背景和使用场景衡量摘要的信息满意度偏好。

Result: 包括LLM评判在内的多种流行指标无法通过基础的信息内容扰动测试，表明它们对信息差异不敏感。专家评估显示，传统指标和基于LLM的指标均不足以衡量信息满意度，与人类判断的一致性差。

Conclusion: 现有摘要评估指标无法有效衡量摘要对特定用户的信息满足程度，需要发展能够感知用户背景和persona的评估方法。

Abstract: The majority of work on summarization evaluation focuses on general summary quality (e.g., ROUGE, BERTScore) or specific desired properties (e.g., readability, factuality). However, these metrics fail to measure the utility of a summary to an individual user. For example, a biomedical researcher learning about the latest vaccine research will have different informational needs from a family doctor. Query-focused summarization captures part of this need, but in practice, users rarely state everything relevant in a query: a single short query is likely inadequate to distinguish the needs of a researcher from those of a physician. By contrast, a reader's background or persona (their role and expertise) is comparatively stable across queries and recovers much of this missing context, which makes it a practical signal for assessing whether a summary satisfies that reader's needs. In this work, we assess how sensitive popular summarization metrics are to both informational and persona differences, and find that many popular metrics, including strong LLM-as-judge metrics, fail basic perturbation tests of informational content. We additionally conduct an expert human evaluation, measuring summary preferences based on information satisfaction given a specific person's background and use case. We find that both traditional and LLM-based metrics are insufficient measures of information satisfaction and agree poorly with human judgment.

</details>


### [99] [You Only Pass Once: Answering and Abstaining Together in a Single Forward Pass of a Frozen Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14465)
*Ziyang Luo,Zhongyao Chu,Xinjie He,Youting Wang,Xukui Qin,Runxiong Wu,Yan-Syuan Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: YOPO (You Only Pass Once) enables a frozen LLM to simultaneously improve reasoning via a steering probe and detect insufficient evidence for abstention via a sufficiency direction, all in a single forward pass. It trains a lightweight reconstructor to undo the steering-induced shift on the residual stream so the abstention direction can be read on the cleaned representation, avoiding the 2-pass overhead and interference of the naive combination. Experiments on Qwen2.5 (1.5B–7B) and ten other backbones show three-way (answer-correct, answer-wrong, abstain) accuracy more than doubles the frozen baseline and beats the two-pass reference at every scale; a new answer-or-abstain benchmark is contributed.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Frozen LMs on reasoning tasks suffer two coupled weaknesses: they under-utilize evidence already encoded in their residual stream, and they fail to detect when input is insufficient, leading to confabulation. Prior work addressed each separately (steering probe for reasoning, sufficiency direction for abstention) but combining them in one forward pass causes interference (the steering write shifts the state the direction reads), while a two-pass solution doubles inference cost. The goal is to achieve both steering and abstention in a single forward pass without loss of accuracy or calibration.

Method: YOPO freezes the backbone (Qwen2.5 1.5B/3B/7B, plus ten others). It keeps a zero-shot sufficiency direction fixed and trains a small reconstructor network to map the steered residual (after a conditional steering probe writes to mid-layer) back to the pre-steering residual, using only MSE on (steered, clean) pairs (no sufficiency labels). The sufficiency direction is then read on this reconstructed residual, enabling abstention decisions. This avoids interference and the need for a separate clean forward pass.

Result: End-to-end three-way accuracy more than doubles the frozen baseline (0.375→0.798 on 1.5B alphaNLI). The single-pass YOPO beats the two-pass reference at every scale (0.798/0.830/0.893 vs 0.753/0.790/0.863 for 1.5B/3B/7B). Results hold across ten backbones from six model families. On the contributed answer-or-abstain benchmark (four domains), the YOPO gate tops every in-domain dataset and the label-free direction is the only gate family that survives domain transfer. A source-side audit caught a surface artifact in their alphaNLI construction, so architectural claims are anchored on native-label replications (SQuAD2, RepLiQA, MuSiQue).

Conclusion: YOPO demonstrates that a frozen LM can steer its own reasoning and abstain when necessary in a single forward pass, achieving substantial gains over both the frozen baseline and a two-pass combination. The principle that abstention should not be trained in is quantified via a capacity-transfer frontier. The label-free abstention direction generalizes across domains, making YOPO a practical and principled approach for safe reasoning in frozen models.

Abstract: A frozen language model on reasoning tasks has two coupled weaknesses: it under-uses evidence its own residual stream already encodes, and it fails to detect when the input is insufficient to answer, so it confabulates. This paper consolidates two research lines that address these on the same residual stream: a conditional steering probe writes the stream at mid-stack layers and recovers reasoning accuracy from a frozen backbone, and a zero-shot sufficiency direction reads the stream and abstains when information is insufficient. Deployed in one forward pass they interfere: the steering write shifts the state the direction reads, costing up to 8 AUROC points of cross-domain transfer on small models; a separate clean pass doubles inference cost. We keep the direction fixed and train a small network to reconstruct the pre-steering residual from the steered one -- mean-squared error on (steered, clean) pairs, no sufficiency labels -- and read the direction on the reconstruction. The resulting system, YOPO (You Only Pass Once), answers, steers, and abstains in one forward pass of a frozen Qwen2.5 backbone (1.5B/3B/7B). End to end, three-way accuracy more than doubles the frozen baseline (0.375->0.798 on 1.5B alphaNLI) and one pass beats the two-pass reference at every scale (0.798/0.830/0.893 vs 0.753/0.790/0.863) and on ten backbones across six model families. We chart the capacity-transfer frontier quantifying the principle that abstention should not be trained in; a source-side audit catches our own alphaNLI construction leaking a surface artifact, so architectural claims are anchored on native-label replications (SQuAD2, RepLiQA, MuSiQue); and on the standard four-domain suite we contribute, to our knowledge, the first answer-or-abstain benchmark, where our gate tops every in-domain dataset and the label-free direction is the only gate family to survive domain transfer.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] [L-FNO: Lorentzian Fourier Neural Operator for Stochastic Event Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13562)
*Songhee Kang,Jihoon Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: L-FNO 是一种结合 FNO 风格协变量路径、洛伦兹谱核和似然训练目标的随机神经算子，专门用于处理稀有、突发和自激事件的时间点过程建模，在合成和真实数据上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准神经算子通常作为回归式函数到函数模型训练，而非条件强度估计器，限制了它们在稀疏事件场景（如稀有、突发和自激事件）中的适用性。

Method: L-FNO 将 FNO 风格的协变量路径与洛伦兹谱核（用于历史依赖的激发）相结合，并采用基于似然的训练目标进行优化。

Result: 在 8 个合成点过程基准和 3 个真实数据集（疾病爆发预测和半导体故障检测）上，L-FNO 在事件似然、校准诊断和稀有事件检测方面均优于回归式和基于似然的神经算子基线。

Conclusion: 结构化的谱记忆和基于似然的学习为神经算子模型在随机事件动态中提供了有效的归纳偏置。

Abstract: Modern operational systems face uncertainty even in routine conditions, where rare, bursty, and self-exciting events emerge from both exogenous covariates and endogenous event dynamics. Standard neural operators are typically trained as regression-style function-to-function models rather than conditional-intensity estimators, limiting their suitability for sparse event regimes. We introduce the Lorentzian Fourier Neural Operator (L-FNO), a stochastic neural operator that combines an FNO-style covariate path, Lorentzian spectral kernels for history-dependent excitation, and a likelihood-based training objective. We evaluate L-FNO on eight synthetic point-process benchmarks and three real-world datasets covering disease outbreak prediction and semiconductor fault or defect detection. L-FNO improves event likelihood, calibration diagnostics, and rare-event detection over regression- and likelihood-based neural operator baselines. These results show that structured spectral memory and likelihood-based learning provide effective inductive biases for neural operator models of stochastic event dynamics.

</details>


### [101] [Don't Claim Benchmark-Oriented Optimization Improves General Coding Capability -- Diverse Evaluation Is Required](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13566)
*Egor Shibaev,Vera Kudrevskaia,Timur Galimzyanov,Mikhail Evtikhiev,Ana Terna,Rastislav Rabatin,Timur Kudashev,Timofey Bryksin,Arina Puchkova,Patrik Bartak,Egor Bogomolov,Sergey Titov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出，当前后训练论文、模型卡片和博客文章常将少量编码基准（如SWE-bench和LiveCodeBench）上的分数视为广泛编码能力的证据，但针对这些基准的优化实际上衡量的是任务特定性能，造成了测量分数与通用编码能力声称之间的意义鸿沟。作者通过Django案例研究基准套件验证了这一差距，发现基准排名经常无法泛化，后训练检查点几乎不存在跨任务迁移，SWE-bench优化在作者的任务或LiveCodeBench上几乎没有或完全没有收益。作者得出结论，少数基准不足以评估基准优化压力下的多样化模型，并鼓励社区采用差异化评估——前沿模型进行全面评估，研究使用多任务套件，窄任务应用进行人机交互研究。最后，作者主张创建能力分类法和持续的基准维护，而非一次性基准发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前后训练论文、模型卡片和博客文章常将少量编码基准（SWE-bench、LiveCodeBench）上的分数视为广泛编码能力的证据，但针对这些基准的优化会导致任务特定性能，造成测量分数与通用编码能力声称之间的意义鸿沟，导致工程师和研究人员不得不依赖不充分的证据做出研究、开发和部署决策。

Method: 作者创建了一个基于Django的案例研究基准套件，评估了在后训练SWE-bench轨迹上的基础模型和检查点，检验基准排名是否泛化、跨任务迁移情况以及SWE-bench优化在作者任务和LiveCodeBench上的效果。同时测试了在单个Django模态上微调后的迁移效果。

Result: 基准排名经常无法泛化；后训练检查点几乎不存在跨任务迁移；SWE-bench优化在作者的任务或LiveCodeBench上几乎没有或完全没有收益；在单个Django模态上微调也无法迁移到其他模态。

Conclusion: 少数基准不足以评估基准优化压力下的多样化模型。鼓励社区采用差异化评估（前沿模型进行全面评估，研究使用多任务套件，窄任务应用进行人机交互研究），并主张创建能力分类法和持续的基准维护，而非一次性基准发布。缺乏可靠的评估标准会导致不充分的证据支持研究、开发和部署决策。

Abstract: Post-training papers, model cards, and blog posts often treat scores on a small set of coding benchmarks (e.g., SWE-bench and LiveCodeBench) as evidence of broad coding capability, both for research artifacts and user-facing systems. We argue that optimization for these benchmarks leads to measuring task-specific performance, creating a meaning gap between measured scores and claims of general coding ability. We examine this gap with a Django-based case study benchmark suite we create.
  Evaluating foundation models and checkpoints post-trained on SWE-bench trajectories, we find that benchmark rankings frequently fail to generalize. Post-trained checkpoints show little cross-task transfer, and SWE-bench optimization yields limited or no gains on our tasks or on LiveCodeBench. Similarly, fine-tuning on individual Django modalities fails to transfer.
  We conclude that a small number of benchmarks is insufficient for evaluating diverse models under benchmark optimization pressure. We encourage the community to use differentiated evaluation - holistic assessment for frontier models, multi-task suites for research, and human-in-the-loop studies for narrow task applications. Finally, we argue for creating a capability taxonomy and sustained benchmark maintenance, rather than one-off benchmark releases. Without reliable evaluation standards, engineers and researchers using LLMs and agents have to rely on insufficient evidence to make research, development, and deployment decisions.

</details>


### [102] [Robust XGBoosting for Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13590)
*Iris Aragón Mladosich,Christophe Croux*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper studies the robustness of XGBoost against vertical outliers and leverage points, and proposes MM-XGBoost—a two-step procedure using M-, S-, and τ-estimators from robust regression—that achieves the best trade-off between robustness and prediction accuracy.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XGBoost is widely used but its performance can be degraded by vertical outliers and leverage points; standard loss functions (quadratic, Huber) are not sufficiently robust.

Method: The authors explore alternative loss functions based on M-, S-, and τ-estimators from robust regression, and develop a two-step procedure called MM-XGBoost.

Result: MM-XGBoost provides the best trade-off between robustness and prediction accuracy compared to standard XGBoost with quadratic or Huber loss.

Conclusion: The MM-XGBoost approach effectively mitigates the impact of vertical outliers and leverage points, making XGBoost more robust without sacrificing predictive performance.

Abstract: XGBoost is a very popular and powerful method for prediction. It iteratively fits simple decision trees to the residuals of the previous step. An efficient and scalable implementation is available. The standard loss function for XGBoost is the quadratic loss, but a Huber loss can also be used. In this paper, we study the robustness of XGBoost and show that its performance can be affected by vertical outliers and leverage points. To address this, we explore alternative loss functions, based on M-, S-, and τ -estimators from robust regression. Our results indicate that a two-step procedure, referred to as MM-XGBoost, provides the best trade-off between robustness and prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [103] [Training-Free Knowledge Transfer Across Model Scales through Activation-Guided Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13596)
*Jiahe Fan,Si Chen,Yinghao Hou,Aiyuan Zhang,Hong Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出激活-剪枝-融合（APM）框架，通过激活图引导从强捐赠模型中选择功能组件并注入弱接收模型，在无需显式语义对齐的情况下实现跨尺度异构模型融合，在16个基准上将3B接收模型平均准确率从55.5%提升至60.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索异构模型融合中一个未被充分研究的跨尺度场景：在存在显著架构不匹配的情况下，将更强的捐赠模型的能力转移到较小的接收模型中，且无需显式的神经元级语义对齐。

Method: 提出Activation-Prune-Merge (APM)框架：(1) 在捐赠模型上构建任务条件激活图；(2) 根据激活图选择显著层、隐藏维度、注意力头和MLP神经元，将捐赠模型剪枝为接收模型架构；(3) 使用微插值系数将剪枝后的捐赠切片注入原始接收模型。

Result: 在16个基准（涵盖推理、数学、代码生成、指令跟随和分类）上，APM将原始3B接收模型的平均准确率从55.5%提升至60.6%。其中RTE准确率从64.3%提升至82.3%，QNLI从52.3%提升至65.7%，BoolQ从70.8%提升至79.2%。

Conclusion: 跨尺度异构融合可以在无需显式语义对齐的情况下成功，前提是捐赠模型的贡献足够集中且经过精心选择。激活引导的提取能够提高可迁移捐赠切片的质量，同时保持小比例融合机制的优势。

Abstract: Heterogeneous model fusion seeks to combine models that differ in tasks, initializations, architectures, or scales. We study an underexplored cross-scale setting: improving a small recipient language model with a stronger donor despite substantial architectural mismatch. We ask whether useful capabilities can be transferred without explicit neuron-wise semantic alignment. Building on the observation that truncating a large model to a smaller architecture and injecting it with a tiny mixing weight can already improve the recipient, we propose Activation-Prune-Merge (APM), an activation-guided framework for cross-scale fusion. APM constructs task-conditioned activation maps on the donor, selects salient layers, hidden dimensions, attention heads, and MLP neurons to prune it to the recipient architecture, and injects the resulting donor slice into the original recipient using a micro interpolation coefficient. This formulation treats the donor as a source of concentrated functional components rather than requiring precise structural transplantation. Across 16 benchmarks spanning reasoning, mathematics, code generation, instruction following, and classification, APM improves the overall average accuracy from 55.5% to 60.6% over the original 3B recipient. RTE accuracy increases from 64.3% to 82.3%, QNLI from 52.3% to 65.7%, and BoolQ from 70.8% to 79.2%. Analyses of injection ratios and sequential multi-stage fusion further suggest that activation-guided extraction improves the quality of the transferable donor slice while preserving the small-ratio fusion regime. These results provide evidence that cross-scale heterogeneous fusion can succeed without explicit semantic alignment when the donor contribution is sufficiently concentrated and carefully selected.

</details>


### [104] [Hard Cases, Bad Labels: Testing Error Exposure and Error Location in Uncertainty Sampling Under Bounded Label Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13601)
*John Myron Uy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了不确定性采样在主动学习中是否因获取更多错误标签或因错误集中在困难区域而失效。通过对比基于边际的不确定性采样与随机采样，在三个公开二值表格数据集上，考虑干净标签、随机分类噪声和有界困难依赖噪声。实验使用100对种子、9个噪声率(0-0.30)、标注预算20-120，逻辑回归并每步重新选择正则化。暴露匹配的RCN控制组平衡了最终获取的污染，干净标签扩展至预算400。干净标签下不确定性采样将归一化平衡准确率学习曲线下面积提升了1.09-1.77个百分点。在乳腺癌数据集上，困难依赖噪声比RCN更严重地削弱了优势，但在其他数据集上未观察到。暴露匹配分析未发现错误位置结构化带来额外惩罚的普遍证据。在干净MAGIC数据上，不确定性采样提升了平衡准确率但降低了平均精度和固定假阳性率下的真阳性率。因此，不确定性采样具有标签效率，但其鲁棒性取决于数据集、预算、噪声结构和评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动学习通过选择信息量最大的样本来减少标注成本，但最不确定的样本也可能最难正确标注。本研究旨在测试不确定性采样失败的原因：是因为它获取了更多错误标签，还是因为错误集中在困难区域尤其有害。

Method: 对比基于边际的不确定性采样与随机采样，在三个公开二值表格数据集（Breast Cancer Wisconsin, Banknote Authentication, MAGIC Gamma Telescope）上进行实验。考虑干净标签、随机分类噪声（RCN）和有界困难依赖噪声。使用100对随机种子、9个预期噪声率(0-0.30)、标注预算20-120，逻辑回归模型并在每个预算步骤通过交叉验证重新选择正则化参数。设计了暴露匹配的RCN对照组以平衡最终获取的污染程度，并在干净标签下将预算扩展至400。

Result: 干净标签下，不确定性采样将归一化平衡准确率学习曲线下面积提升了1.09-1.77个百分点。在Breast Cancer Wisconsin数据集上，困难依赖噪声在6/8的噪声率下比RCN更显著地削弱了不确定性采样的优势；但在Banknote Authentication和MAGIC Gamma Telescope数据集上未观察到这一现象。暴露匹配分析未发现错误位置结构化带来额外惩罚的普遍证据。在干净MAGIC数据上，不确定性采样提高了平衡准确率，但降低了平均精度和固定假阳性率下的真阳性率。

Conclusion: 不确定性采样在标签效率上表现良好，但其鲁棒性依赖于数据集、标注预算、噪声结构和评估指标。没有证据表明错误位置的结构化会普遍导致额外惩罚。

Abstract: Active learning can reduce labeling cost by selecting informative examples, but the most uncertain examples may also be the hardest to label correctly. This study tests whether uncertainty sampling fails because it acquires more corrupted labels or because errors concentrated in difficult regions are especially harmful. Margin-based uncertainty sampling is compared with random sampling under clean labels, random classification noise (RCN), and bounded difficulty-dependent noise on three public binary tabular datasets. The design uses 100 paired seeds, nine expected noise rates from 0 to 0.30, annotation budgets from 20 to 120, and logistic regression with regularization re-selected by cross-validation at every budget. An exposure-matched RCN control aligns mean final acquired corruption, while a clean-label extension reaches budget 400. Under clean labels, uncertainty sampling improved normalized balanced-accuracy area under the learning curve by 1.09 to 1.77 percentage points on all datasets. Difficulty-dependent noise reduced this advantage more than RCN at six of eight rates on Breast Cancer Wisconsin, but at no tested rate on Banknote Authentication or MAGIC Gamma Telescope. Exposure-matched analyses found no corrected evidence for a universal additional penalty from structured error location. On clean MAGIC data, uncertainty sampling improved balanced accuracy while reducing average precision and true-positive rate at fixed false-positive rates. Thus, uncertainty sampling was label-efficient, but its apparent robustness depended on dataset, budget, noise structure, and evaluation metric.

</details>


### [105] [Robust Dual-Model Collaborative Random Vector Functional Link Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13628)
*A. Quadir,A. Rahaman,Mushir Akhtar,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于核风险敏感均值p-功率准则的随机向量功能连接网络（KRPRVFL），通过替换标准最小二乘目标为KRP损失并引入协作学习机制，增强模型对噪声标签、异常值和数据不平衡的鲁棒性，在UCI和KEEL基准上取得更优的分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RVFL模型对噪声标签、异常值和类别不平衡数据敏感，限制了其在真实场景中的性能，亟需一种兼顾RVFL计算效率与鲁棒性的改进方法。

Method: (1) 将RVFL的标准最小二乘损失替换为核风险敏感均值p-功率（KRP）准则，自适应降低受损或不可靠样本的影响；(2) 引入协作学习机制，实现模型组件间的自适应交互以增强鲁棒性；(3) 采用核诱导特征映射捕捉非线性关系，避免显式隐层选择，保持效率与可扩展性。

Result: 在UCI和KEEL基准数据集上的大量实验表明，KRPRVFL在准确性、鲁棒性和统计显著性方面持续优于基线模型。

Conclusion: KRPRVFL是一种快速、可扩展且可靠的高噪声环境分类方案，有效结合了RVFL的计算效率与核风险敏感准则的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Random vector functional link (RVFL) networks are lightweight and fast neural models that offer efficient training and strong generalization through randomized hidden-layer weights and direct input-output connections. However, conventional RVFL models are sensitive to noisy labels, outliers, and imbalanced data, which limits their performance in real-world applications. To address these challenges, we propose the kernel risk-sensitive mean p-power based RVFL (KRPRVFL) model, which integrates the computational efficiency of RVFL with the robustness of the kernel risk-sensitive mean p-power (KRP) criterion. By replacing the standard least-squares objective with a KRP-based loss, KRPRVFL adaptively reduces the influence of corrupted or unreliable samples during training, resulting in improved stability and generalization. Additionally, a collaborative learning mechanism is introduced to enable adaptive interaction among model components, further enhancing robustness in complex and noisy environments. The proposed framework also leverages kernel-induced feature mapping to capture nonlinear relationships without requiring explicit hidden-layer selection, maintaining both efficiency and scalability. Extensive experiments on UCI and KEEL benchmark datasets demonstrate that KRPRVFL consistently outperforms baseline models in terms of accuracy, robustness, and statistical significance, highlighting its effectiveness as a fast, scalable, and reliable solution for challenging classification tasks.

</details>


### [106] [Contrastive Learning for Interpretable Anomaly Detection at Collider Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13652)
*Haoyi Jia,Sagar Addepalli,Julia Gonski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ORCA是一个两阶段框架，先通过监督对比学习在多样物理过程上学习嵌入空间，再在该空间中运行标准自编码器生成事件级异常分数。在HL-LHC模拟数据上，ORCA相比基线自编码器显著提升了对新物理信号的灵敏度，且对比嵌入使得异常样本可解释：通过最大似然模板拟合嵌入分布，可将异常事件归因于已知物理过程并量化不确定性，还能恢复未参与训练的信号产额并描述未知信号。这为可解释的异常检测碰撞机搜索提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碰撞物理中通用事件级异常检测存在两个反复出现的问题：异常分数难以解释，且与能量尺度和物体多重性强烈相关。现有方法缺乏可解释性，限制了异常检测在碰撞机搜索中的应用。

Method: ORCA采用两阶段框架：第一阶段通过监督对比学习在多样物理过程上学习一个嵌入空间，使得不同物理过程在空间中被组织到不同区域；第二阶段在该嵌入空间上运行标准自编码器，生成事件级异常分数。异常样本的可解释性通过最大似然模板拟合实现，将嵌入分布拟合到已知物理过程的模板分布，从而量化每个事件的来源归属。

Result: 在模拟的HL-LHC条件下，ORCA相对于基线自编码器架构在灵敏度的广度和深度上均有显著提升。对比嵌入使得异常样本可解释：模板拟合能准确恢复注入的信号产额（包括训练中未见的信号），并能通过已知过程的相似性来表征模板库中缺失的信号。嵌入几何携带了更高维的物理信息，增强了后续统计分析。

Conclusion: ORCA建立了碰撞机中可解释的异常检测搜索途径，其嵌入几何携带的高维物理信息优于标准一维输出拟合，能够提升下游统计分析能力，为未来高亮度大型强子对撞机上的新物理搜索提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Generic event-level anomaly detection for collider physics has two recurring problems: anomaly scores are hard to interpret, and they correlate strongly with energy scale and object multiplicity. We present Organized Representation via Contrastive learning for Anomaly detection (ORCA), a two-stage framework that first learns an embedding space via supervised contrastive learning across a diverse set of physics processes, then runs a standard autoencoder in that space to generate event-level anomaly scores. On a simulated dataset consistent with conditions at the High-Luminosity Large Hadron Collider, ORCA delivers significant gains in both breadth and depth of sensitivity to new physics signals with respect to a baseline autoencoder architecture. Beyond improved sensitivity, the contrastive embedding makes the anomalous sample interpretable: because known processes occupy distinct regions of the space, a maximum-likelihood template fit to the embedding distributions can attribute events in an anomalous sample to template physics processes with quantified uncertainties. We demonstrate that the fit accurately recovers injected signal yields, including for signals excluded from the training of the embedding, and characterizes signals absent from the template library through the known processes they most resemble. These results establish ORCA as a route to interpretable anomaly detection-based searches at colliders, where the embedding geometry carries higher dimensional physics information compared to standard one-dimensional output fits, enhancing downstream statistical analysis.

</details>


### [107] [The Query Knows What to Forget: A Second Erase Direction for Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13668)
*Dhruman Gupta,Aritra Das,Debayan Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出查询导出擦除方向（Query-derived Erase Direction, QED），通过增加一个由查询导出的正交擦除方向来改进线性注意力中的Delta规则，减少状态干扰，从而提升长上下文检索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力采用固定大小的状态，长上下文下多个存储项共享该状态，导致干扰并降低检索质量。现有Delta规则模型（如GDN-2）虽然使用擦除向量，但仅从键（key）导出，无法处理通过查询（query）测量的读取干扰。

Method: 提出QED，在原有键擦除方向基础上，增加一个从查询导出且与键正交的第二擦除方向。在快速权重视角下，键导向的Delta编辑无法改变读取中与键正交的部分，而QED利用可编辑部分抵消沿查询方向测量的旧状态内容。

Result: QED在训练窗口外的所有长度上均提升了检索性能，在S-NIAH-1任务上将有效上下文长度大约翻倍。

Conclusion: QED通过引入查询导出的正交擦除方向，有效缓解了线性注意力中固定大小状态导致的干扰问题，显著提升了长上下文场景的检索能力。

Abstract: Linear attention keeps a state of fixed size. At long context, many stored items share this state, and interference between them degrades retrieval. Gated DeltaNet-2 (GDN-2), like every delta-rule model before it, derives its erase vector from the key of the current token. However, the interference in its reads is measured through the query, and the erase step cannot reach it. We introduce the Query-derived Erase Direction (QED). QED adds a second erase direction derived from the query and orthogonal to the key. In the fast-weight view, a key-directed delta edit cannot change the key-orthogonal part of a read. It uses the editable part to cancel old-state content measured along the query. It also improves retrieval at every length past the training window, and it about doubles the usable context length on S-NIAH-1.

</details>


### [108] [From BERT to Frontier Agents: Eight Years of Language-Model Progress, the Collapse of the Capability-Cost Curve, and the Rise of Task-Targeted Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13675)
*Pranav Kumar Kaliaperumal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 从2018年10月到2026年7月，AI模型从BERT等简单系统发展为能解决复杂数学和编写软件的大型智能体。自2024年底以来，解决实际编码问题的能力每年提升近6倍，成本急剧下降（OpenAI的GPT 5.6 Luna以每百万token仅1-6美元的价格匹配旗舰能力）。顶级性能现在分散在多个专业模型上：Claude Opus 5领跑前端编码，Claude Fable 5擅长仓库级编码，GPT 5.6 Sol主导终端任务。在小学数学测试中，Qwen 2.5的基础方法解决58/100题，高级采样解决高达79题。置信度排序工具在前50个选择中正确识别了47个正确答案，所有研究材料已完全公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 追踪AI模型从BERT到2026年大型智能体的快速进展，评估不同模型在编码、数学推理等任务上的性能提升和成本效益，并探索置信度排序工具的有效性。

Method: 对比不同时期AI模型（BERT到GPT 5.6、Claude Opus 5/Fable 5等）在编码任务、数学测试（Qwen 2.5基础方法与高级采样）上的表现，并引入置信度排序工具评估其识别正确答案的能力。

Result: 编码能力每年提升近6倍；GPT 5.6 Luna以极低成本匹配旗舰性能；专业模型各有所长；高级采样在数学测试中提升21个百分点；置信度排序工具在前50选择中准确率达94%。

Conclusion: AI模型在2018-2026年间实现了飞跃式发展，性能和成本效益大幅提升，专业化分工明显，置信度排序工具具有实用价值，所有研究材料已开源。

Abstract: Between October 2018 and July 2026 AI models progressed from simple systems like BERT to massive agents that solve complex math and write software. The ability to resolve real coding issues improved by nearly six times per year since late 2024. During this time costs dropped sharply with OpenAIs budget model GPT 5 point 6 Luna matching flagship capabilities for just one to six dollars per million tokens beating older versions at a fraction of the price. Top performance is now split across specialized models as Claude Opus 5 leads in frontend coding Claude Fable 5 excels at repository level coding and GPT 5 point 6 Sol dominates terminal tasks. In a grade school math test using the Qwen 2 point 5 model basic methods solved 58 of 100 problems while advanced sampling solved up to 79. A confidence ranking tool correctly identified 47 right answers in its top 50 choices proving highly useful for sorting tasks with all research materials made fully public.

</details>


### [109] [EEG-PRISM: Physiologically-Grounded Interpretability of Predictions by EEG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13676)
*Deeksha M Shama,Punnisa Amornsirikul,Archana Venkataraman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EEG-PRISM is a post-hoc attribution method that maps time-channel attribution scores from EEG foundation models into physiologically relevant domains (frequency and source) using linear transformations and backpropagation rules, enabling accurate spectral recovery, seizure onset localization, and biomarker identification without retraining.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current explainable AI for EEG foundation models provides attribution scores in the time-channel space, which is mismatched to clinical intuition. A universal method is needed to extend interpretability to alternative, physiologically relevant domains without modifying the underlying model.

Method: EEG-PRISM uses linear transformations and backpropagation rules to map time-channel attribution scores into frequency domain via invertible DFT and into source domain via approximately invertible EEG generative model. It is evaluated on simulated and real data with five foundation models and four AI explainers.

Result: Near-perfect spectral recovery in simulation, 69.2% spatial accuracy; in epilepsy, correctly identifies delta-theta as most salient and localizes seizure onset with 50% accuracy; in autism, localizes predictive delta-alpha biomarkers to frontal and temporal regions.

Conclusion: EEG-PRISM is a theoretically-grounded post-hoc attribution method that accurately maps into spectral and spatial domains, supporting window-level analysis of transient events and group-level identification of clinically relevant biomarkers, advancing interpretable EEG foundation models.

Abstract: Objective: Foundation models represent the next advancement in AI for EEG analysis; however current explainable AI techniques provide attribution scores in the time-channel input space, which is mismatched to clinical intuition about EEG. Thus, there is a critical need for a universal method that can extend the interpretability of any foundation model to alternative and physiologically relevant domains without modifying or retraining the underlying model. Methods: EEG-PRISM leverages linear transformations and established backpropagation rules to map time-channel attribution scores into alternative domains. We derive mappings to the frequency domain via an invertible DFT and to the source domain via an approximately invertible EEG generative model. We evaluate EEG-PRISM in simulated and real data, assessing recovery of ground-truth phenomena across domains with five foundation models and four AI explainers. Results: In simulation, EEG-PRISM achieves near-perfect spectral recovery and 69.2% spatial accuracy. In epilepsy, EEG-PRISM correctly determines that delta-theta activity is most salient and correctly localizes the seizure onset region with 50% accuracy. In autism, EEG-PRISM localizes the predictive delta-alpha biomarkers to frontal and temporal regions, consistent with prior work. Conclusion: EEG-PRISM is a theoretically-grounded post-hoc attribution method with accurate mapping into the spectral and spatial domains. It supports window-level analysis of transient events (e.g., seizures) and group-level identification of clinically relevant biomarkers (e.g., autism), thus advancing interpretable EEG foundation models. Significance: This work enables physiologically-grounded interpretation of EEG foundation models and supports clinically relevant insights such as event localization and biomarker identification.

</details>


### [110] [SAGE: Surrogate-gradient Adaptation via Attention-Guided Entropy for Spiking Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13702)
*Kiran Nair,Rodrigue Rizk,KC Santosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGE，一种基于不确定性调制替代梯度的机制，用于Transformer架构的脉冲神经网络（SNN）训练。SAGE利用自注意力熵计算块级不确定性，动态调整训练时的替代梯度斜率，在不改变推理模型的前提下提升优化灵活性。实验表明，在CIFAR-10/100上，SAGE相比固定替代梯度基线可获得1-2%的精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲神经网络（SNN）虽然能效优越，但其训练因脉冲函数的非可微性而困难，常用的替代梯度方法采用固定形状的替代函数，这可能在不同层和训练阶段并非最优，限制了训练效果。

Method: 提出SAGE方法，通过归一化的自注意力熵估计块级不确定性，并利用该信号在训练过程中动态调整替代梯度的斜率；推理时保持原模型不变，仅修改训练时的替代参数。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/100数据集上，SAGE相比固定替代梯度基线，在多个仿真时间步下一致取得1-2%的精度提升。

Conclusion: 注意力机制导出的不确定性可作为轻量级训练信号，用于Transformer-based SNN的自适应替代梯度学习，提升训练效果且不增加部署成本。

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs) offer an energy-efficient alternative to conventional deep neural networks by exploiting sparse event-driven computation, but their training remains challenging because the non-differentiable spike function requires surrogate gradients whose fixed shape may be suboptimal across layers and training stages. In this work, we introduce SAGE, an uncertainty-modulated surrogate-gradient mechanism for Transformer-based SNNs. SAGE estimates block-level uncertainty from normalized self-attention entropy and uses this signal to adapt the surrogate-gradient slope during training while leaving the inference model unchanged.
