By modulating only the training-time surrogate parameter, the proposed method preserves the original architecture and deployment cost while improving optimization flexibility. Experiments on CIFAR-10/100 demonstrate that SAGE achieves improved accuracy over fixed-surrogate baselines, with results up to 1-2\% consistent gains across multiple simulation time steps. These results highlight the potential of attention-derived uncertainty as a lightweight training signal for adaptive surrogate-gradient learning in transformer-based SNNs.

</details>


### [111] [Capacity-Dependent Effects of Data Selection for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13721)
*Cuong Dang,Hoang Anh Just,Ruoxi Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: The paper shows that the effectiveness of likelihood-based data selection in reasoning supervised fine-tuning depends on model capacity and training duration. High-likelihood responses benefit smaller models early on, while low-likelihood responses become more beneficial for larger models with longer training, challenging the universal preference for high-likelihood supervision.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To challenge the prevailing assumption that high-likelihood responses (closer to the student's current distribution) are universally preferable for fine-tuning reasoning tasks, by investigating how model capacity and training duration affect the value of likelihood-based data selection.

Method: Controlled experiments on mathematical reasoning with student models ranging from 1.5B to 8B parameters, using supervision from stronger teacher models. Analysis of learning dynamics and a capacity-constrained theoretical view of distillation.

Result: Observes a capacity-dependent "Fast-Fit / Slow-Gain" pattern: high-likelihood data yields faster early improvements (especially for smaller models), while low-likelihood data becomes increasingly beneficial for larger models given longer training. Small models fail to absorb low-likelihood supervision, whereas larger models can better utilize it.

Conclusion: Effective data selection for reasoning should be aware of model capacity and computing budget rather than relying on a single universal preference for high-likelihood supervision.

Abstract: In reasoning supervised fine-tuning, candidate responses for the same instruction can differ substantially in how well they match the student's current distribution. Recent likelihood-based response selection methods suggest that responses closer to the student distribution provide more effective supervision, motivating the hypothesis that high-likelihood responses may generally be preferable for fine-tuning. In this paper, we revisit this intuition and show that the value of likelihood-based data selection depends critically on model capacity and training duration. Through controlled experiments on mathematical reasoning, using students ranging from 1.5B to 8B parameters and supervision generated by stronger teacher models, we observe a clear \emph{capacity-dependent} ``{\color{SMALLCOLOR}\textbf{Fast-Fit}} / {\color{LARGECOLOR}\textbf{Slow-Gain}}'' pattern. High-likelihood data provides faster and more stable early improvements, especially for smaller models, but low-likelihood data becomes increasingly beneficial for larger models when training is allowed to continue longer. To explain this phenomenon, we analyze learning dynamics, showing that small models often fail to absorb low-likelihood supervision and instead fall into shallow or repetitive behaviors, while larger models are better able to move toward the teacher distribution under such data. We further provide a capacity-constrained theoretical view of distillation that clarifies how data difficulty, data span, and student capacity jointly govern transfer. Overall, our findings show that effective data selection for reasoning should be aware of model capacity and computing budget rather than based on a single universal preference for high-likelihood supervision.

</details>


### [112] [The Integer Alibi: Localizing Cross-Kernel Divergence in INT8-Quantized LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13756)
*Teng-Ruei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性地测试了GPU上实现相同scaled INT8 GEMM接口的两个内核（CUTLASS vs Triton）是否可互换。在固定所有其他条件（检查点、提示、硬件、推理引擎、解码、量化配置）下，仅替换vLLM中的INT8线性内核，发现两个内核在冷重启后各自能逐比特复现自身，但在端到端比较中没有任何序列达成一致（0/8, 0/16, 0/64）。通过验证INT32点积精确且与顺序无关（无溢出），定位分歧源于缩放应用和输出舍入。在幂次缩放尺度下获得逐比特一致，确认了逐层预测；在真实缩放尺度下差异最多一个bfloat16精度。相同的干预作为探针检查点可恢复端到端逐比特一致。FP8 GEMM呈现不同特征：差异流行度和幅度随归约深度增长。教师强制重放将层与令牌关联，翻转集中在小的logit边际上（ROC-AUC 0.94）。作者将发布预注册、逐层预测、清单和一致性检查程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两个实现相同scaled INT8 GEMM接口的GPU内核通常被视为可互换的。本文挑战这一假设，通过严格控制变量（检查点、提示、硬件、推理引擎、解码、量化配置均固定），仅替换vLLM中的INT8线性内核（CUTLASS vs Triton），观察端到端行为是否一致，以揭示内核实现差异对推理结果的影响。

Method: 在vLLM中仅替换INT8 GEMM内核（CUTLASS vs Triton），其余条件完全固定。通过验证INT32点积在无溢出条件下精确且与顺序无关，将分歧定位到缩放应用和输出舍入。使用幂次缩放尺度获得逐比特一致，确认逐层预测；对真实缩放尺度分析差异幅度。通过教师强制重放分析翻转与logit边际的关系。还比较了FP8 GEMM的不同特征。

Result: 两个内核在端到端比较中在所有序列上均不一致（0/8, 0/16, 0/64）。在幂次缩放尺度下逐比特一致，确认了196/196和252/252的逐层预测；在真实缩放尺度下差异最多一个bfloat16精度。相同的干预（探针检查点）可恢复端到端逐比特一致（8/8, 16/16）。FP8 GEMM差异随归约深度增长，INT8差异率仅为百万分之几且在一个精度内。翻转集中在小的logit边际上，ROC-AUC达0.94。

Conclusion: 内核并非可互换；分歧源于缩放应用和输出舍入而非累加器。作者将发布预注册、逐层预测、清单（含内核选择证据）以及一个一致性检查程序，将上述控制转化为具体的可互换性检验。

Abstract: Two GPU kernels implementing the same scaled INT8 GEMM interface are usually treated as interchangeable. We test that assumption: holding the checkpoint, prompts, hardware, inference engine, decoding, and quantization configuration fixed, we swap only the INT8 linear kernel (CUTLASS versus Triton) inside vLLM. At 1.7B each arm reproduces itself bit-for-bit across cold restarts, yet the arms agree on no sequence in any end-to-end comparison we ran (0/8, 0/16, and 0/64). What makes this more than a benchmark discrepancy is an integer alibi: for shared INT8 operands under a verified no-overflow bound, the INT32 dot product is exact and order-independent, so the accumulator cannot be the source of any difference. Feeding both kernels identical operands from every linear layer of Qwen3-1.7B and 8B (196 and 252 layers), we find bit-identical outputs under power-of-two scales, confirming a pinned prediction list 196/196 and 252/252 (pre-registered at 1.7B, pinned but not blind at 8B), and observed differences of at most one bfloat16 spacing under the checkpoints' real scales. This localizes the divergence to scale application and output rounding after the exact accumulator. Applied as a probe checkpoint, the same intervention restores end-to-end bitwise agreement (8/8 and 16/16 sequences). Cross-implementation FP8 GEMM shows a different signature: both the prevalence and the magnitude of differences grow with reduction depth, while the INT8 fraction stays at parts per million and within one spacing over a 64x range of K. Teacher-forced replay ties layers to tokens: flips concentrate at small logit margins, which predict flip risk with ROC-AUC 0.94 on 16,384 positions. We will release the pre-registration, per-layer predictions, manifests with kernel-selection evidence, and a conformance procedure that turns these controls into a concrete check for kernel interchangeability.

</details>


### [113] [CutClean: Neural Network Pruning for Privacy-Preserving Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13773)
*Leonardo Magliolo,Vito Paolo Pastore,Giuseppe Valenzise,Enzo Tartaglione*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CutClean，一种通过剪枝减少神经网络中隐私信息泄露的方法，在增加网络稀疏性的同时降低隐私信息流动，实验表明该方法能有效保护隐私并维持任务精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络在高风险应用中面临隐私泄露风险，且这种泄露可能在没有传统数据集偏差的情况下发生，说明需要新的隐私保护方法。

Method: CutClean 是一种隐私感知剪枝方法，通过在网络每个模块放置辅助线性隐私头来量化信息泄露，然后逐步增加稀疏度以移除隐私属性泄露（基于最后一个模块隐私头的准确率）。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，CutClean 能有效最小化隐私信息流动，同时实现高稀疏率并保持分类目标精度。

Conclusion: CutClean 通过剪枝在保护隐私、提升稀疏性和维持任务性能之间取得良好平衡，为隐私敏感场景下的模型部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Neural networks are increasingly deployed in high-stakes applications with growing privacy leakage concerns. We show that this privacy leakage can occur even in the absence of representation imbalances that lead to traditional dataset biases. This poses significant privacy risks when deploying models that process sensitive attributes. In this context, we propose CutClean, a privacy-aware pruning method that allows to reduce privacy information flow through the network, while increasing its sparsity. Our approach employs auxiliary linear privacy heads placed at each network's block to quantify information leakage, and further applies increasing levels of sparsity to remove the private attribute leakage, measured in terms of the accuracy of the privacy head attached to the last block. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that our approach effectively minimizes private information flow while achieving high sparsity rates and preserving classification target accuracy.

</details>


### [114] [PPAPlace: Differentiable Cross-Stage Objectives for Chip Placement Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13790)
*Ruogu Chen,Jie Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PPAPlace is a timing-driven differentiable surrogate for post-route PPA prediction from macro and standard-cell placements. It uses a dual-stream predictor (graph attention + spatial convolution) trained on post-global-routing labels. The gradients of predicted WNS/TNS are used to guide placement optimization via co-objective injection in an analytical placer (PPAPlace-CoOpt) or post-placement refinement via projected gradient descent (PPAPlace-Refine). On five test circuits, it improves WNS by 22% and TNS by 51% over hierarchical baseline while preserving power and routability.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing placement methods optimize HPWL which has near-zero correlation with post-route timing metrics (WNS, TNS). All six evaluated AI placers degraded PPA relative to hierarchical baseline. Prior cross-stage predictors focus on macro-only representations and use pre-route metrics as training labels, which poorly reflect final timing. A label fidelity study shows post-global-routing achieves the best balance between timing fidelity and cost-effectiveness, motivating a differentiable surrogate trained on such labels to directly optimize post-route PPA.

Method: PPAPlace consists of: (1) A dual-stream predictor combining graph attention over the chip netlist with spatial convolution over the placement grid, trained on post-global-routing labels to predict post-route WNS and TNS. (2) Two usage modes: PPAPlace-CoOpt injects the predicted gradients as a co-objective into an analytical placer's optimization loop; PPAPlace-Refine adjusts macro positions via projected gradient descent using the surrogate's gradients. The gradients flow end-to-end back to cell coordinates.

Result: On five ChiPBench test circuits excluded from training, PPAPlace improves average WNS by 22% and TNS by 51% over the hierarchical baseline while preserving power and routability, using the same predictor without test-circuit retraining.

Conclusion: PPAPlace demonstrates that a differentiable surrogate trained on post-global-routing labels can effectively bridge the gap between placement optimization and post-route timing, significantly improving WNS and TNS without sacrificing power or routability, and without requiring retraining on test circuits.

Abstract: Macro placement significantly affects a chip's post-route performance, power, and area (PPA). Most placement methods optimize half-perimeter wirelength (HPWL) as the primary objective. However, recent benchmarking shows a near-zero correlation between HPWL and post-route timing metrics such as the worst negative slack (WNS) and total negative slack (TNS). As a result, all six evaluated artificial intelligence (AI) placers degraded PPA relative to the hierarchical baseline. Recent efforts have tried to train cross-stage predictors to close this gap. However, existing methods focus on macro-only representations and use pre-route metrics as training labels. A label fidelity study of ten circuits at four design flow stages reveals that HPWL and pre-route timing poorly reflect final post-route timing rankings. In contrast, post-global-routing achieves the best balance between final timing fidelity and label generation cost-effectiveness. Based on this finding, PPAPlace is a timing-driven differentiable surrogate predicting post-route PPA from macro and standard-cell placements. The surrogate is a dual-stream predictor that combines graph attention over the chip netlist with spatial convolution over the placement grid. It is trained on post-global-routing labels. The predicted WNS and TNS gradients flow end-to-end back to cell coordinates. PPAPlace exploits these gradients in two ways: as a co-objective injected into an analytical placer's optimization loop (PPAPlace-CoOpt), and as a post-placement refinement step that adjusts macro positions via projected gradient descent (PPAPlace-Refine). On five ChiPBench test circuits excluded from training, PPAPlace improves average WNS and TNS by 22\% and 51\% over the hierarchical baseline while preserving power and routability, using the same predictor without test-circuit retraining. Code is available at https://github.com/ValleyC/PPAPlace.

</details>


### [115] [Recent Advances in Deep Learning-Based Drug-Target Binding Affinity Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13797)
*Jafin Khan,Md Hossain Shuvo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文对药物-靶标结合亲和力预测的机器学习方法进行了全面综述与比较分析，重点评估了现有深度学习方法在标准基准上的表现及其局限性，包括数据集偏差、评估不充分、冷启动问题等，并提出了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物-靶标结合亲和力预测是药物发现中的关键子问题，虽然已有许多机器学习方法，但准确预测仍具挑战。现有方法在标准基准上表现良好，但存在数据集偏差、评估设置有限、冷启动场景下性能下降等问题，亟需系统性的综述与分析以识别研究空白。

Method: 论文采用综述与分析的方法：回顾了代表性的深度学习方法，涵盖多种神经网络架构和表示策略；分析了七个广泛使用的基准数据集和常用评估指标；从数据集偏差、冷启动场景等角度评估方法性能；归纳了当前方法的局限性并讨论了未来研究方向。

Result: 分析表明，许多方法在标准基准上报告了强性能，但其有效性常受数据集偏差和有限评估设置影响；大多数方法在冷启动场景下性能下降，暴露出泛化挑战；当前方法存在数据集不平衡、缺乏标准化评估、现实适用性有限等问题。

Conclusion: 论文指出了未来的研究方向，包括改进数据集设计、建立更鲁棒的评估方法、更好地处理冷启动问题以及整合多模态表示，以推动药物-靶标结合亲和力预测领域的进一步发展。

Abstract: Computational approaches to drug discovery involve multiple sub-problems, and among them, drug-target binding affinity prediction plays an important role. Despite recent advances, accurately predicting binding affinity remains an open research area. The major objective of our paper is to perform a comprehensive review and comparative analysis of recent machine learning methods for drug-target binding affinity prediction, with a focus on identifying strengths, limitations, and research gaps. We review representative recent deep learning approaches that use common benchmark datasets and evaluation metrics, covering a range of neural network architectures and representation strategies. In addition, we analyze seven widely used benchmark datasets and commonly adopted evaluation metrics for drug-target binding affinity prediction. Our analysis indicates that although many methods report strong performance on standard benchmarks, their effectiveness is often influenced by dataset bias and limited evaluation settings. Furthermore, most methods exhibit reduced performance in cold-start scenarios, highlighting challenges in generalization. We identify several limitations of current approaches, including dataset imbalance, the lack of standardized evaluation, limited real-world applicability, and challenges in cold-start scenarios. We also discuss future research directions, including better dataset design, more robust evaluation methods, improved handling of cold-start problems, and the integration of multimodal representations.

</details>


### [116] [Dynamic Multi-Depot Vehicle Routing with Online Requests: Event-Driven Transformer--DRL and Rolling-Horizon Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13799)
*Faezeh Ardali,Gerald M. Knapp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个事件驱动的学习和基准测试框架，用于解决动态多仓库车辆路径问题（动态MDVRP），该问题涉及逐步揭示的请求和不断演变的车辆状态。通过行为克隆和近端策略优化（PPO）训练了带掩码的MLP和Transformer策略。确定性可行性掩码防止无效的车辆-请求分配，固定前缀/灵活后缀的路线承诺保护已完成、活跃和近期的决策，并分别衡量车辆重分配和重排序。学习策略与动态插入启发式和有限时域滚动优化进行了比较。在20个场景的策略基准测试中，所有方法均完成了所有请求且无无效动作，但最近可行启发式取得了最低的平均目标值，并在路线质量、等待时间、稳定性、完工时间和运行时间上优于学习策略。在五次独立训练中，PPO对MLP的平均影响很小，但对Transformer有平均提升，尽管种子变异性更大。在共同协议下，最近可行启发式实现了最低的组合目标和路线干扰，而滚动优化在显著更高的计算成本下实现了最低的等待时间和完工时间。学习策略保持了毫秒级的决策速度，并可迁移至多达80个请求的实例而无需重新训练，但未能超越最强的启发式方法。没有单一方法在路线效率、服务响应性、稳定性和在线计算方面都是最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态VRP方法通常无法同时处理多仓库环境、渐进式请求揭示、车辆状态动态变化以及在线决策的实时性要求，缺乏统一的基准测试框架来公平比较学习型策略与传统方法。

Method: 提出事件驱动的学习和基准测试框架；使用掩码MLP和Transformer策略，通过行为克隆和PPO训练；引入确定性可行性掩码和固定前缀/灵活后缀路线承诺机制；与动态插入启发式和滚动时域优化对比。

Result: 最近可行启发式在多数指标上最优；PPO对Transformer有平均提升但种子变异性大；学习策略速度快但未超越最强启发式；滚动优化效果好但计算成本高；无单一方法全面最优。

Conclusion: 在动态多仓库VRP中，传统启发式（最近可行）在当前设定下优于学习型策略，但学习策略在计算效率和迁移性方面具有优势；未来需在路线效率、服务响应性、稳定性和在线计算之间寻求更好的平衡。

Abstract: This paper presents an event-driven learning and benchmarking framework for the Dynamic Multi-Depot Vehicle Routing Problem with progressively revealed requests and evolving vehicle states. Masked MLP and Transformer policies are trained through behavior cloning and proximal policy optimization. Deterministic feasibility masking prevents invalid vehicle--request assignments, while fixed-prefix/flexible-suffix route commitments protect completed, active, and near-term decisions and separately measure vehicle reassignment and resequencing. The learned policies are compared with dynamic insertion heuristics and time-limited rolling-horizon optimization. In a 20-scenario policy benchmark, all methods completed every request without invalid actions, but nearest feasible achieved the lowest mean objective and outperformed the learned policies in routing quality, waiting time, stability, makespan, and runtime. Across five independent training runs, PPO had little average effect on the MLP and improved the Transformer on average, although with greater seed variability. Under the common protocol, nearest feasible achieved the lowest combined objective and route disruption, whereas rolling horizon achieved the lowest waiting times and makespan at substantially higher computational cost. The learned policies retained millisecond-level decisions and transferred to instances with up to 80 requests without retraining, but did not outperform the strongest heuristic. No single method was best across routing efficiency, service responsiveness, stability, and online computation.

</details>


### [117] [Stochastic Control Policies for Robust Molecular Transition Path Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13800)
*Jingqian Liu,Yu-Hsiang Wang,Yanru Qu,Ge Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于机器学习的过渡路径采样（TPS）方法，特别是依赖于显式分子动力学（MD）展开的基于rollout的控制方法。作者将此类方法重新解释为在路径空间上学习提议分布，并提出两种随机策略（FS-TPS和LaS-TPS）来引入随机性，从而提高探索能力和优化鲁棒性。在三种生物分子系统（丙氨酸二肽、chignolin和BBL蛋白）上的多种子实验表明，随机策略一致地提高了过渡成功率和路径质量，同时显著降低了对随机初始化的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于rollout的控制方法（即在显式MD轨迹中学习控制力）虽然能生成物理上更合理的轨迹，但存在不稳定和强烈依赖于随机种子的问题。作者旨在通过重新设计策略中的随机性放置来改善探索和优化鲁棒性。

Method: 作者将rollout-based控制重新理解为在路径空间上学习提议分布，并将随机性的放置视为关键设计选择。提出了两种随机策略：FS-TPS直接对控制策略输出参数化一个状态相关的高斯分布；LaS-TPS则采样一个紧凑的潜在控制变量，并将其解码为具有跨原子相关性的结构化力变化。

Result: 在丙氨酸二肽、chignolin和BBL蛋白三种不同规模的生物分子系统上进行的多种子实验表明，与确定性策略基线相比，随机策略一致地提高了过渡成功率和路径质量，并显著降低了对随机初始化的敏感性。

Conclusion: 随机策略能够提升TPS方法的鲁棒性和性能，通过在策略中引入适当的随机性可以改善探索和优化过程。

Abstract: Transition path sampling (TPS) aims to efficiently generate rare molecular transition trajectories between metastable states and is essential for understanding biomolecular mechanisms. Beyond traditional molecular dynamics (MD)-based sampling, machine learning has become central to state-of-the-art TPS. One major class of methods learns control forces during explicit MD rollouts. By preserving the underlying molecular dynamics, these methods tend to produce more physically plausible trajectories than endpoint-conditioned generators that construct paths directly. However, rollout-based control methods have been reported to exhibit unstable and strongly seed-dependent performance. We recast rollout-based control as learning a path-space proposal distribution and investigate stochasticity placement as a design choice for improving exploration and optimization robustness. We develop two stochastic policies: FS-TPS, which directly parameterizes a state-dependent Gaussian distribution over the control policy output, and LaS-TPS, which samples a compact latent control variable and decodes it into structured, cross-atom-correlated force variation. We conduct extensive multi-seed experiments on three biomolecular systems of increasing size: alanine dipeptide, chignolin, and BBL, a fast-folding protein. Stochastic policies consistently improve transition success and path quality over deterministic-policy baselines while substantially reducing sensitivity to random initialization.

</details>


### [118] [HI-MeshGraphNets: Efficient and Accurate Mesh-based Physics Learning with Hierarchical Multi-scale Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13827)
*SiHun Lee,Dong-Hyuk Park,Taesoo Bang,Seung-Hoon Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HI-MGN is a multiscale extension of MeshGraphNets that uses hierarchical message passing with coarse graph coarsening (farthest-point sampling + Voronoi partitioning) and learned interpolation to efficiently handle long-range interactions on large, high-fidelity unstructured meshes, achieving better accuracy, lower training time, and reduced memory usage.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Conventional flat message passing in GNNs becomes inefficient on large, high-fidelity meshes because information propagates only one hop per layer, requiring deep processors for long-range interactions, which increases computational cost, memory usage, and the risk of over-smoothing.

Method: HI-MGN replaces the flat processor with a hierarchical multiscale processor that coarsens graphs using farthest-point sampling and Voronoi partitioning while preserving the original mesh topology. Message passing on coarse graphs enables long-range communication with fewer layers, and a learned graph interpolation network reconstructs fine-resolution features.

Result: Across three structural and fluid benchmarks, HI-MGN achieves improved accuracy compared to MeshGraphNets and Bi-Stride Multi-Scale GNN while reducing training time and peak memory usage.

Conclusion: Topology-aware hierarchical message passing and learned coarse-to-fine interpolation provide an effective and practical framework for scalable mesh-based physics surrogate modeling.

Abstract: Machine-learned physical surrogate models have become promising alternatives to mesh-based numerical solvers. Among them, graph neural networks (GNNs) are well suited for representing simulation meshes and learning nodal state evolution through message passing. However, conventional flat message passing becomes inefficient on large, high-fidelity meshes because information propagates only one hop per layer, requiring deep processors for long-range interactions and increasing computational cost, memory usage, and the risk of over-smoothing.
  To address this limitation, we propose Hierarchical Interpolating MeshGraphNets (HI-MGN), a multiscale extension of MeshGraphNets for efficient long-range communication on unstructured meshes. HI-MGN replaces the flat processor with a hierarchical multiscale processor that coarsens graphs using farthest-point sampling and Voronoi partitioning while preserving the original mesh topology. Message passing on coarse graphs enables information to travel over larger geometric distances with fewer layers, and a learned graph interpolation network reconstructs fine-resolution features.
  Across three structural and fluid benchmarks, HI-MGN achieves improved accuracy compared with MeshGraphNets and the Bi-Stride Multi-Scale GNN while reducing training time and peak memory usage. The results show that topology-aware hierarchical message passing and learned coarse-to-fine interpolation provide an effective and practical framework for scalable mesh-based physics surrogate modeling.

</details>


### [119] [Federated Prompt Learning: A Unified Framework, Empirical Analysis, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13844)
*Qinglin Yang,Chen Qiu,Hongyuan Zhang,Pengdeng Li,Yuan Liu,Zhihong Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了联邦提示学习（FPL）在隐私保护的大型语言模型（LLM）训练与推理中的最新进展，系统回答了FPL的基本动机、特性、与传统FL及全模型微调的区别、性能与效率的权衡，以及安全、隐私和鲁棒性挑战与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在云端服务中计算成本高、数据集中且存在隐私风险，而联邦学习（FL）提供了一种无需共享原始数据的去中心化训练范式。论文旨在通过综述联邦提示学习（FPL），探索如何将FL与LLM有效结合以解决隐私保护问题，并厘清该领域的核心研究问题。

Method: 采用系统性综述方法，从完整模型生命周期（预训练、微调及实际应用）出发，梳理现有FPL方法，同时讨论安全、隐私和鲁棒性问题并总结现有防御机制。

Result: 明确了FPL相较于传统FL和全模型联邦微调的根本差异；揭示了FPL方法在性能、通信效率、计算开销、可扩展性、个性化及异构处理方面的权衡；指出了剩余的安全、隐私、鲁棒性和系统挑战。

Conclusion: 联邦提示学习是隐私保护LLM训练与推理的可行方向，但仍面临多重挑战，未来需在安全、隐私、鲁棒性及系统优化方面进一步研究。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become core components of cloud-based intelligent services in academia and industry, yet their training and deployment are hindered by high computational costs, data centralization, and privacy concerns. Federated learning (FL) offers a decentralized training paradigm that enables clients to collaboratively train a learning model without sharing raw data, making it a promising solution for privacy-preserving LLM training and reasoning. This paper presents a comprehensive survey of federated prompt learning (FPL) to review recent advances in integrating the federated learning paradigm and large language models, answering the following research questions: RQ1: The fundamental motivations, characteristics, and enabling technologies of FPL, and how it differs from conventional FL and full-model federated fine-tuning; RQ2: The trade-offs FPL approaches exhibit in performance, communication efficiency, computational overhead, scalability, personalization, and heterogeneity handling; RQ3: The remaining security, privacy, robustness, and system challenges, along with key future research directions. To this end, we systematically examine existing FPL methods across the full model lifecycle: pre-training, fine-tuning, and practical applications, while discussing security, privacy, and robustness issues and summarizing existing defense mechanisms. Finally, we highlight open challenges and future directions, aiming to help readers understand how the insights drive research in FPL.

</details>


### [120] [Geometric Filtering of LLM-Generated Samples for Few-Shot Text Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13866)
*Benjamín Schindler,Gonzalo A. Ruz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种几何过滤框架，通过计算LLM生成样本与真实类别样本在句子嵌入空间中的欧氏距离，选择几何一致的样本并赋予软权重，用于文本分类的数据增强。在13个数据集、5个分类器、10种增强方法、超过6700种配置下，该方法相比SMOTE平均提升2.61个百分点（p<0.0001, d=0.95, 胜率88.9%），并能泛化到命名实体识别任务（+9.26pp, 100%胜率），且最简单的距离过滤优于复杂多准则方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的合成训练数据质量参差不齐，部分样本落入正确的类别区域，而其他样本位于边界或跨类别区域，需要一种有效筛选高质量合成样本的方法。

Method: 提出几何过滤框架：将LLM生成的样本映射到句子嵌入空间，计算其与真实类别样本的欧氏距离，选取几何一致的候选样本；并通过软加权机制将过滤分数转换为样本权重，用于分类器训练。

Result: 在13个数据集、5个分类器、10种增强方法和超过6700种配置下，该方法相比SMOTE平均提升2.61个百分点（p<0.0001, Cohen's d=0.95, 胜率88.9%）。在命名实体识别任务上提升9.26个百分点（100%胜率）。在5种不同LLM上均表现鲁棒。且最简单的距离过滤优于复杂多准则方法。

Conclusion: 基于欧氏距离的几何过滤框架可以有效筛选LLM生成的合成样本，提升文本分类和命名实体识别的性能，且简单距离过滤优于复杂方法，具有较好的泛化性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate synthetic training data for text classification, but the quality of generated samples is heterogeneous: some fall in correct class regions of the embedding space while others land in peripheral or cross-class zones. We propose a geometric filtering framework that evaluates each LLM-generated sample by its Euclidean distance to real class examples in a sentence embedding space, selecting only geometrically consistent candidates. A soft weighting mechanism transforms filter scores into sample weights for classifier training. Evaluated across 13 datasets, 5 classifiers, 10 augmentation methods, and over 6,700 configurations, our method achieves +2.61 percentage points (pp) over SMOTE ($p<0.0001$, Cohen's $d=0.95$, 88.9% win rate). The approach generalizes to named entity recognition (+9.26pp, 100% win rate) without filter modification, and is robust across 5 LLMs from 4 providers. A key finding is that the simplest distance-based filter consistently outperforms complex multi-criteria alternatives.

</details>


### [121] [Variation Brownian Kernel Ladders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13882)
*Mahdi Mohammadigohari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出变分布朗核阶梯（VBKL），一种路径原子函数空间框架，将非线性递归字典构建与线性变分叠加分离。通过布朗再生核希尔伯特空间的单位球轮廓递归组合，构建变分可控的函数空间，并证明了深度增长、紧性、达到性以及泛化误差界，为深度表示学习的复杂度-精度权衡提供了理论支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于深度收益的论断依赖于表示复杂度的分配方式，缺乏将非线性递归字典学习与线性变分分离的通用理论框架。本文旨在建立一种路径原子函数空间理论，清晰解释深度为何以及如何在有限数据下带来更好的精度-复杂度权衡。

Method: VBKL框架从线性投影开始，每个原子通过布朗RKHS的单位球轮廓递归组合，形成递增字典；完整空间则是该字典的带符号测度变分包。通过将递归字典视为布朗拉回RKHS球的并集，建立变分控制的Hölder正则性、紧性、达到性及深度严格增长条件。利用布朗二次混沌、符号阈值迹和VC熵导出Rademacher与泛化界；并通过离散化外测度与外布朗轮廓构造两阶段逼近器，得到M^{-1/2}+m^{-1/2}误差界和锐利插值常数√(A/2)，每次评估最多2M个活跃基。

Result: 理论结果包括：变分控制的Hölder正则性、紧性与达到性；局部非退化条件下深度严格增长；基于布朗二次混沌的Rademacher与泛化界；两阶段逼近的M^{-1/2}+m^{-1/2}误差上界及锐利插值常数√(A/2)；每次评估最多2M个活跃外轮廓基。实验验证了近似机制，并展示了有限数据下有利的精度-复杂度折中。

Conclusion: VBKL框架将深度收益归因于递归字典构建与变分叠加的分离，理论分析与实验结果表明该方法在有限数据场景下具有良好的近似效率与泛化能力，为深度表示学习提供了新的函数空间视角。

Abstract: Claims about the benefit of depth depend on the complexity assigned to a representation. We introduce the \emph{Variation Brownian Kernel Ladder} (VBKL), a path-atomic function-space framework that separates nonlinear recursive dictionary construction from linear variation superposition. Starting from linear projections, each atom recursively composes unit-ball profiles from the Brownian reproducing kernel Hilbert space; the full VBKL space is then the signed-measure variation hull of the completed dictionary. We identify each recursive dictionary as a union of Brownian pullback RKHS balls and establish variation-controlled Hölder regularity, compactness and attainment, and strict growth with depth under a local non-degeneracy condition whose trace lies in the support of the input measure. For associated finite lower-support architectures, we derive Rademacher and generalization bounds through Brownian quadratic chaos, signed threshold traces, and VC entropy. We also construct two-stage approximants by discretizing the outer measure and the selected outer Brownian profiles, obtaining an $M^{-1/2}+m^{-1/2}$ error bound, a sharp interpolation constant $\sqrt{A/2}$, and at most $2M$ active outer-profile basis contributions per evaluation. Controlled experiments illustrate the approximation mechanisms and indicate a favorable limited-data accuracy--complexity trade-off.

</details>


### [122] [Fashion Outfit Generation via Unified Sequential Composition Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13888)
*Kaicheng Pang,Xingxing Zou,Ruohan Xu,Waikeung Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对时尚穿搭生成任务，将其形式化为约束集合生成（CEG）并建模为有限时域确定性马尔可夫决策过程。提出统一序列组合模型（USCM）联合建模集合级兼容性和潜在组合意图，并基于USCM学习先验提出潜在扩张蒙特卡洛树搜索（LE-MCTS）来平衡局部美学与全局结构。在Polyvore Outfits等数据集上实现最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时尚穿搭生成面临美学兼容性的非单调性和隐式特性以及巨大的组合搜索空间，是一个非平凡的挑战。本文将任务形式化为约束集合生成（CEG），旨在从海量单品库中合成风格协调的穿搭。

Method: 将CEG建模为有限时域确定性马尔可夫决策过程；提出统一序列组合模型（USCM）联合建模集合级兼容性和潜在组合意图；提出潜在扩张蒙特卡洛树搜索（LE-MCTS），利用USCM学习先验来指导单品检索，平衡局部美学协同和全局结构平衡。

Result: 在Polyvore Outfits数据集上进行大量实验，并在iFashion和PolyvoreU数据集上进行零样本评估，在独立人类偏好评估、自动美学代理和结构有效性指标上均达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架在约束时尚穿搭生成任务中实现了最先进的性能，有效解决了美学兼容性和组合空间挑战。

Abstract: The task of synthesizing stylistically coherent fashion outfits from massive item libraries, known as fashion outfit generation, remains a non-trivial challenge, primarily due to the non-monotonic and implicit nature of aesthetic compatibility, coupled with the exponentially large combinatorial search space. In this paper, we formalize this task as Constrained Ensemble Generation (CEG) and model it as a finite-horizon deterministic Markov Decision Process. To address CEG in fashion, we propose the Unified Sequential Composition Model (USCM), which jointly models set-level compatibility and latent composition intents. Guided by USCM's learned priors, a Latent Expansion Monte Carlo Tree Search (LE-MCTS) mechanism is proposed to handle item retrieval during composition, balancing local aesthetic synergy with global structural balance. Extensive experiments on the Polyvore Outfits dataset, along with zero-shot evaluations on the iFashion and PolyvoreU datasets, demonstrate that our framework achieves state-of-the-art performance across independent human preference evaluations, automated aesthetic proxies, and structural validity metrics for constrained fashion outfit generation.

</details>


### [123] [MedMix: Specialization-Consistent Federated Sparse MoEs under Modality Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13911)
*Adiba Orzikulova,Dong Min Kim,Jaehong Yoon,Sung-Ju Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MedMix is a semantic-alignment framework for federated multimodal sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) that addresses modality heterogeneity at client and sample levels. It introduces modality-context-aware routing, consensus-guided routing alignment, and client-adaptive expert aggregation to coordinate cross-client routing and expert specialization. Experiments on real-world medical datasets show superior F1 scores under varying modality heterogeneity and incompleteness.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Federated multimodal medical AI suffers from modality heterogeneity: clients may systematically lack certain modalities and individual records contain partial modal subsets. Sparse MoE architectures are promising for modality-adaptive computation but fail under cross-client heterogeneity because local routing policies diverge, causing experts to specialize incompatibly and standard aggregation to misalign or overwrite intended specialization.

Method: MedMix proposes three components: (1) Modality-context-aware routing at the client side, which uses each token's modality identity, position, and incompleteness context to guide expert selection. (2) Consensus-guided routing alignment across clients, which builds server-side consensus anchors for shared modality patterns and aligns local routing distributions to them. (3) Client-adaptive expert aggregation, which uses client-specific modality-pattern prototypes to match and aggregate functionally similar experts across clients.

Result: On real-world multimodal medical datasets, MedMix achieves the best average F1 score across diverse modality heterogeneity and modality incompleteness settings, with especially clear gains under severe heterogeneity.

Conclusion: The proposed MedMix effectively mitigates the fragility of sparse MoE in federated settings, enabling robust multimodal learning despite heterogeneous and incomplete modality configurations across clients. The semantic-alignment approach preserves expert specialization and improves model accuracy, particularly under challenging heterogeneity conditions.

Abstract: Federated multimodal medical AI faces modality heterogeneity at both the client and sample levels: clients may systematically lack access to specific modality types, while individual records within the same client may contain different partial modality subsets. Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures are a promising remedy for modality-adaptive computation, but their use in federated learning is fragile under cross-client modality heterogeneity, where locally learned routing policies can diverge across clients and drive experts toward incompatible specializations. Different clients may assign the same observed modality configuration to different experts, or train similarly indexed experts on different missing-modality configurations, causing standard aggregation to misalign or overwrite the expert specialization that sparse MoEs are intended to learn. To address this challenge, we propose MedMix, a semantic-alignment framework for federated multimodal sparse MoEs that coordinates cross-client routing and expert specialization using modality context. At the client side, MedMix uses modality-context-aware routing to guide expert selection using each token's modality identity, position, and incompleteness context. Across clients, it uses consensus-guided routing alignment to construct server-side consensus anchors for shared modality patterns and align local routing distributions across clients. Complementing these routing mechanisms, client-adaptive expert aggregation leverages client-specific modality-pattern prototypes to match and aggregate functionally similar experts across clients. Experiments on real-world multimodal medical datasets show that MedMix achieves the best average F1 across diverse modality heterogeneity and modality incompleteness settings, with especially clear gains under severe heterogeneity.

</details>


### [124] [Hybrid Quantum-inspired Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks for Privacy-Aware Federated Biosignal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13914)
*Chun-Hua Lin,Samuel Yen-Chi Chen,Yu-Chao Hsu,Kuo-Chung Peng,Jiun-Cheng Jiang,Chi-Sheng Chen,Tai-Yue Li,Nan-Yow Chen,En-Jui Kuo,Hsi-Sheng Goan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了混合量子启发的Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（HQKAN）与多层感知机（MLP）在联邦学习框架下进行心电信号（ECG）心律失常分类的性能。在MIT-BIH数据集（五分类）和INCART数据集（三分类）上，HQKAN在多数指标（尤其是少数类）上优于MLP，同时使用更少的可训练参数（减少37.35%-44.81%）并降低通信开销（24.89%-36.41%），表明HQKAN是隐私保护联邦学习中一种紧凑、高效且鲁棒的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ECG记录是敏感的生物医学数据，限制了医院和可穿戴设备共享原始信号进行集中式模型训练。联邦学习虽能解决隐私问题，但面临客户端样本有限、心律失常标签不平衡以及跨客户端数据非独立同分布（non-IID）等挑战，需要既通信高效又对跨客户端分布偏移鲁棒的分类器。

Method: 提出混合量子启发的Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（HQKAN），在联邦平均（FedAvg）框架下与MLP基线进行对比，在MIT-BIH数据集上进行五类心律失常分类，在INCART数据集上进行三类分类，评估多个客户端配置下的性能。

Result: HQKAN在多数聚合指标和少数类指标上优于MLP，同时在MIT-BIH上可训练参数减少37.35%、通信成本降低24.89%；在INCART上参数减少44.81%、通信成本降低36.41%。

Conclusion: HQKAN为隐私感知的联邦学习生物信号数据提供了一种紧凑、通信高效且鲁棒的替代方案，优于MLP基线。

Abstract: Electrocardiogram (ECG) recordings are sensitive biomedical data, limiting the ability of hospitals and wearable devices to share raw signals for centralized model training. Federated learning addresses this practical privacy constraint by enabling collaborative model training while keeping raw biosignal data at their respective sources. However, federated ECG classification remains challenging due to limited client-side samples, imbalanced arrhythmia labels, and non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID) data across clients. These constraints require classifiers that are both communication-efficient and robust to cross-client distribution shifts. In this work, we evaluate a hybrid quantum-inspired Kolmogorov-Arnold network (HQKAN) against a multilayer perceptron (MLP) for five-class arrhythmia classification on the MIT-BIH dataset and three-class classification on the INCART dataset under federated averaging (FedAvg). Across multiple client configurations, HQKAN improves most aggregate and minority-class metrics while using 37.35% fewer trainable parameters and reducing communication cost by 24.89% on MIT-BIH; on INCART, it achieves corresponding reductions of 44.81% and 36.41%. These results indicate that HQKAN offers a compact, communication-efficient and robust alternative to the MLP baseline for privacy-aware federated learning on biosignal data.

</details>


### [125] [High-dimensional nonparametric changepoint detection via low-rank degree-two density projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13922)
*Guoqing Zhang,Zhaixin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于表示的高维分布变化检测方法，通过构造对称特征矩阵H_2(X)保留至多二阶密度信息，将密度估计转化为矩阵均值估计。利用矩阵CUSUM和秩r截断检测低秩投影跳变，而非稀疏坐标跳变。LRD估计量具有帐篷形总体目标函数和非渐近算子范数分析，随机项尺度为√(rd log(nd))。对于多变点，提出种子最窄过阈值过程并通过归纳证明精确恢复。交叉拟合标量精化学习变化低秩方向并定位，达到Õ(κ^{-2})误差，与Le Cam下界匹配（至多对数因子）。在维度高达200、d=100三变化序列及128特征人类活动基准实验中，方法计算高效且能准确检测均值CUSUM无法察觉的纯依赖关系变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维分布变化检测在前后密度均非参数设定时十分困难。现有方法多依赖稀疏性假设，但许多变化体现在依赖结构而非单个坐标均值。需要一种能捕捉低秩依赖变化、避免高维密度估计的通用方法。

Method: 1. 构造对称特征矩阵H_2(X)∈ℝ^{(d+1)×(d+1)}，使其期望M(f)=E_f H_2(X)成为密度二阶正交投影的等距编码。2. 对矩阵CUSUM进行秩r截断，利用投影跳变的低秩性而非坐标稀疏性。3. LRD估计量具有帐篷形总体目标函数，进行非渐近算子范数分析。4. 多变点情形：提出种子最窄过阈值（seed narrowest-over-threshold）过程，通过归纳保持每个未检测变化点的隔离区间，证明精确恢复。5. 交叉拟合标量精化：在一折学习变化低秩方向，另一折定位，达到Õ(κ^{-2})误差。6. 扩展至几何β-混合序列，使用依赖矩阵Bernstein不等式。

Result: 非渐近分析表明随机项以√(rd log(nd))尺度增长。交叉拟合精化达到Õ(κ^{-2})误差，与Le Cam下界匹配（至多对数因子）。实验显示：在d=200维度、d=100三变化序列、128特征人类活动数据上，方法计算可行且能检测纯依赖关系变化（均值CUSUM失效的场景）。

Conclusion: 该方法有效克服高维非参数密度变化检测的维度灾难，通过低秩投影跳变捕捉依赖结构变化，理论达到最优收敛速率（忽略对数因子），且计算实用。为高维分布变化检测提供新范式，尤其适用于均值不变但依赖关系改变的场景。

Abstract: Detecting distributional changes in high dimension is difficult when neither the pre-change nor post-change density is parametrically specified. We introduce a representation-based approach that retains all degree-at-most-two density information while replacing density estimation by matrix mean estimation. For observations in $[-1,1]^d$, a symmetric feature matrix $H_2(X)\in\R^{(d+1)\times(d+1)}$ is constructed so that $M(f)=\E_f H_2(X)$ is an isometric encoding of the degree-two orthogonal projection of the density. We scan matrix CUSUMs after rank-$r$ truncation, exploiting the low rank of the projected jump rather than sparsity of individual coordinates. The resulting \LRD{} estimator has a tent-shaped population objective and a nonasymptotic operator-norm analysis whose leading stochastic term scales as $\sqrt{rd\log(nd)}$. For multiple changes, we give a seeded narrowest-over-threshold procedure and prove exact recovery by an induction that preserves an isolating interval for every undetected change. A cross-fitted scalar refinement learns the changing low-rank direction on one fold and localizes on the other, attaining $\widetilde O_{\Pp}(κ^{-2})$ error; a matching Le Cam lower bound shows optimality up to logarithms. A geometrically $β$-mixing extension follows from a dependent matrix Bernstein inequality. Experiments with ambient dimension up to $200$, a three-change $d=100$ sequence, and a $128$-feature human-activity benchmark show that the method remains computationally practical and accurately detects pure dependence changes that are invisible to mean CUSUMs.

</details>


### [126] [CForce: Boosting Parallel Decoding for dLLMs via Consistency Forcing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13925)
*Yuji Ren,Chenkai Xu,Zhuocheng Gong,Jianguo Li,Zhijie Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Consistency Forcing (CForce) is a distillation method for diffusion LLMs that aligns early-stage mask predictions with later-stage ones using self-rollout trajectories and Confidence Adaptive KL Divergence, improving speed-quality trade-offs especially under high parallelism.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing dLLMs suffer from unreliable predictions in early denoising stages under aggressive parallelism, leading to error propagation. The goal is to improve training-inference alignment and reduce early-stage prediction errors.

Method: CForce is a distillation method that trains the model on pre-collected self-rollout trajectories to force early-stage mask predictions to align with later-stage predictions. It uses Confidence Adaptive KL Divergence as a distillation objective combining forward and reverse KL. Theoretical analysis shows consistency objective approximately minimizes early-stage prediction error. The method applies to both mask-to-token and edit-capable decoding.

Result: Experiments on non-edit and edit-capable LLaDA models show improved speed-quality trade-offs, especially under high-parallelism decoding budgets.

Conclusion: CForce effectively tackles error propagation in dLLMs by enforcing consistency across denoising stages, leading to better performance under aggressive parallelism.

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) accelerate language generation by predicting multiple masks in a single forward pass. However, existing dLLMs can suffer from unreliable predictions in early denoising stages under aggressive parallelism strategies, leading to errors that can propagate to later stages. To tackle this issue, we present Consistency Forcing (CForce) for dLLMs, a distillation method to force the mask predictions of early stages to align with those of later stages. CForce trains the model on pre-collected self-rollout trajectories, thereby improving training-inference alignment. We introduce Confidence Adaptive KL Divergence as a distillation objective to conjoin the merits of forward and reverse KL. We further provide a theoretical analysis for the consistency objective to explain why CForce can approximately minimize the prediction error of early stages. Critically, the same formulation applies to both mask-to-token decoding and edit-capable decoding; in the edit-capable case, later token-to-token refinements provide additional supervision for earlier masked-state predictions. Experiments on non-edit and edit-capable LLaDA models show improved speed-quality trade-offs, especially under high-parallelism decoding budgets. Code is available at: https://github.com/inclusionAI/dFactory.

</details>


### [127] [Post-training Quantization for Hybrid Iterative Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13932)
*Jing Gao,Junyi Wu,Wei Wang,Yan Yan,Yao Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 HyGenQ，一种针对混合迭代生成模型（融合自回归与扩散范式）的后训练量化框架，通过层级聚类解耦（HCD）和缩放重校准（SR）解决激活值中的极端离群点（EOs）和放大异常值（AAs）问题，成功将模型量化至 W8A8 精度且避免模型崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合迭代生成模型推理计算成本高，后训练量化（PTQ）可加速但直接应用会导致模型崩溃。分析发现两个关键挑战：激活值中的极端离群点（EOs）导致正常精度与覆盖离群点之间不可调和的权衡；微小量化误差引发不可预测的放大异常值（AAs），造成校准与推理不匹配并迭代触发模型崩溃。

Method: HyGenQ 包含两个组件：层级聚类解耦（HCD）通过多阶段聚类识别并解耦离群通道，在隔离 EOs 的同时保持正常值精度；缩放重校准（SR）将 AAs 缩放至高斯边界之外，避免激进截断导致的模型崩溃。

Result: 大量实验表明 HyGenQ 成功将代表性混合迭代生成模型量化至 8-bit 精度（W8A8），显著优于现有基线方法，并在不同模型族中验证了其鲁棒性。

Conclusion: HyGenQ 是一种有效的混合迭代生成模型 PTQ 框架，通过针对性解决极端离群点和放大异常值问题，实现了高性能量化且无模型崩溃，为加速这类模型推理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Iterative Generative Models (IGMs) span autoregressive and diffusion paradigms, and hybrid variants that couple them can achieve remarkable image-generation fidelity. However, their iterative inference incurs substantial computational overhead, making Post-training Quantization (PTQ) appealing for acceleration, while directly applying vanilla PTQ to hybrid IGMs can trigger model collapse. By analyzing these failures, we identify two critical challenges: Excessive Outliers (EOs) in the activations create an irreconcilable trade-off between preserving normal precision and covering EOs, resulting in severe degradation in generation quality; Amplified Anomalies (AAs) arising unpredictably from minor quantization errors, create a mismatch between calibration and inference, thus iteratively triggering model collapse. To address these challenges, we introduce HyGenQ, a PTQ framework for hybrid IGMs. HyGenQ comprises Hierarchical Cluster Decoupling (HCD) and Scaling Recalibration (SR). HCD identifies and decouples outlier channels via a multi-stage clustering process, effectively isolating EOs while maintaining normal value precision, thereby alleviating performance degradation. SR scales AAs beyond Gaussian Bound, thereby avoiding model collapse caused by aggressive truncation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HyGenQ successfully quantizes representative hybrid IGMs to 8-bit precision (W8A8), significantly outperforming existing baselines and validating its robustness across different model families.

</details>


### [128] [Probabilistic indirect models for undrained shear strength: addressing significant data missing and variability with advanced imputation and machine learning techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13934)
*Haibin Xiong,Shaoheng Dai,Peng Lan,Xuzhen He,Chenxi Tong,Sheng Zhang,Daichao Sheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用CLAY/10/7490全球数据库，通过比较三种插补方法（MN、MICE、MF）并结合多头注意力机制与概率神经网络（MHA-PNN），开发了预测不排水抗剪强度的概率间接模型，结果表明MN增强的MHA-PNN模型在准确性和不确定性量化上显著优于其他模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统经验方法预测不排水抗剪强度（su）存在较大不确定性，而现有数据集常存在高缺失率和变异性，亟需开发鲁棒的概率间接模型以提高预测精度和可靠性。

Method: 使用CLAY/10/7490数据库，测试MN、MICE、MF三种缺失值插补方法，通过概率极端梯度提升（PXGB）模型评估插补效果；随后构建集成多头注意力机制（MHA）的人工神经网络（ANN）概率模型（MHA-PNN），并与传统MN模型对比，采用RMSE、R²、MAPE、wCI、CR等指标评价。

Result: 所提出的MN增强MHA-PNN模型在预测精度和不确定性量化方面均显著优于其他模型，表明该集成策略在处理稀疏和不完整数据集时具有优越性。

Conclusion: 本研究展示了一种结合插补方法和注意力机制的概率间接建模策略，为岩土工程中面对数据缺失和变异性问题时提供了有效解决方案，具有推广应用潜力。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of undrained shear strength (su) is crucial for geotechnical design, but is often hampered by substantial uncertainty in traditional empirical methods. This study uses the CLAY/10/7490 global database to develop probabilistic indirect models to predict su based on Atterberg limits and piezocone cone penetration (CPTU) measurements. Firstly, the dataset has a high missing data rate and variability. We test three imputation methods - multivariate normal (MN), multiple imputation by chained equations (MICE), and miss forest (MF) - to fill the missing values. To validate their effectiveness, a Probabilistic Extreme Gradient Boosting (PXGB) model is developed, and the imputation methods are evaluated by comparing the PXGB's performance when trained on the imputed datasets against that on the original incomplete data. Secondly, the indirect model is built by integrating a multi-head attention (MHA) mechanism into an artificial neural network (ANN) to enhance information extraction from limited data, which leads to the MHA-based probabilistic neural networks (MHA-PNN) model. The models' performance, alongside a conventional MN-based prediction model, was evaluated using root mean square error (RMSE), coefficient of determination (R2), mean absolute percentage error (MAPE), conditional interval width (wCI), and coverage rate (CR). Results demonstrate that the proposed MN-enhanced MHA-PNN model substantially outperforms other models in both prediction accuracy and uncertainty quantification. These findings highlight the potential of this integrated strategy for building robust probabilistic indirect models in geotechnical applications, particularly when confronted with sparse and incomplete datasets.

</details>


### [129] [Polar Code Based Federated Learning: Convergence Analysis and Resource Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13961)
*Han Xiao,Wei Kang,Nan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于极化码的跨层联邦学习方案，利用极化码在有限块长下的非等错误保护（UEP）特性，选择性地保护更重要的量化比特，从而在真实信道条件下提升联邦学习的鲁棒性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习面临严重的通信瓶颈和信道损伤，传统网络层处理要么将信道理想化为无差错，要么对传输的模型更新采用等错误保护（EEP），忽略了单个本地模型中量化比特内在的不等重要性。

Method: 提出一种基于极化码的跨层FL方案，利用极化码在有限块长下的UEP特性来选择性保护更重要的量化比特；给出了收敛性分析，推导了收敛差距的上界，并联合优化了量化比特数和极化码块长度。

Result: 在恒长和变长块长配置下，该方案均比未编码和基于LDPC的EEP基准方法取得显著的性能提升，且随着信道质量恶化，优势更加明显。

Conclusion: 实验证实了该跨层设计在增强联邦学习在真实信道条件下的鲁棒性和效率方面的有效性。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training across distributed devices without sharing raw data; however, it faces significant communication bottlenecks and channel impairments in practice. Conventional network layer treatments either idealize the channel as error free or apply equal error protection (EEP) to transmitted model updates, failing to account for the inherently unequal importance of quantization bits within a single local model. To address this limitation, we propose a cross layer polar code based FL scheme that leverages the unequal error protection (UEP) property of polar codes under finite block lengths. Specifically, the proposed design selectively protects more significant quantization bits, thereby mitigating the detrimental effects of channel noise. We further provide a rigorous convergence analysis of the proposed scheme, deriving an upper bound on the convergence gap, which we then jointly optimize over the number of quantization bits and the polar code block length across all training iterations. Experimental results demonstrate that both constant and variable block length configurations of our polar code based scheme consistently achieve substantial performance gains over uncoded and LDPC-based EEP benchmarks, with the advantage becoming increasingly pronounced as the channel quality deteriorating. These findings confirm the efficacy of our cross-layer design in enhancing FL robustness and efficiency under realistic channel conditions.

</details>


### [130] [QUASAR: Lowering the Loss Floor of Quantization-Aware Training with Loss-Aware Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13966)
*Vincent Counathe,Ben Athiwaratkun,Christopher De Sa,Tianyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QUASAR是一种量化感知训练（QAT）方法，通过在线、轻量级的损失感知重建来降低低比特大语言模型（LLM）的损失下界，在2/3/4比特下显著优于现有QAT和PTQ方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准QAT中，损失和代理梯度基于有损重建的全精度权重计算，而更新却作用于潜在的全精度权重，这种不匹配导致次优训练轨迹和更高的损失下界。二阶PTQ方法虽然能缓解类似问题，但需要对冻结模型进行数小时的损失感知重建，无法在QAT训练过程中重复使用。

Method: QUASAR在每个训练步中，利用梯度平方的指数移动平均作为在线显著性估计，在小范围内搜索裁剪区间，并通过显著性加权最小二乘法拟合仿射反量化器。该方法仅修改训练过程，支持整数量化和NVFP4等标准部署格式，无需推理时改动。

Result: 在Qwen3和Llama-3.1上，QUASAR在2/3/4比特下均取得最低的留出KL散度，3/4比特KL至少降低10%，2比特降低29%；2比特下八项任务平均准确率比强QAT和PTQ基线提升3.5-4.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 损失感知重建误差是QAT收敛界中唯一依赖重建的项，控制着最终量化模型的损失，QUASAR的目标函数是原则性的优化目标。QUASAR无需推理时开销，可无缝部署。

Abstract: As large language model inference shifts toward lower precision, post-training quantization (PTQ) becomes increasingly brittle, making quantization-aware training (QAT) essential for preserving model quality. However, QAT computes the loss and surrogate gradients using a lossy reconstruction of latent full-precision weights, while applying updates to the latent weights themselves. This mismatch can lead to suboptimal training trajectories and a higher loss floor. Second-order PTQ methods mitigate a similar gap by minimizing loss-aware reconstruction error, but doing it once for a frozen model can take hours; repeating this process throughout QAT as the weights evolve is impractical. We introduce QUASAR, a QAT method that continuously performs lightweight, loss-aware reconstruction in the training loop to lower the loss floor and improve the resulting low-bit model. At each training step, QUASAR uses the exponential moving average of squared gradients as online saliency estimates, searches over a small set of clipping ranges, and fits affine dequantizers via saliency-weighted least squares. Our analysis shows that the loss-aware reconstruction error is the only reconstruction-dependent term in the QAT convergence bound and controls the loss of the final quantized model, establishing QUASAR's objective as a principled optimization target. QUASAR modifies only the training procedure and supports standard deployment formats, including integer quantization and NVFP4, with no inference-time changes or overhead. Across Qwen3 and Llama-3.1, QUASAR achieves the lowest held-out KL divergence among competitive QAT methods at 2, 3, and 4 bits, reducing KL by at least 10% at 3 and 4 bits and by 29% at 2 bits. At 2 bits, it improves average accuracy across eight tasks by 3.5-4.3 percentage points over strong QAT and PTQ baselines.

</details>


### [131] [Identifiability and Order-Dimension Limits of In-Context Learning on Partial Orders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14004)
*Faizanuddin Ansari,Debanjan Dutta,Swagatam Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为偏序集上的上下文学习建立了理论框架，将逻辑可识别性、提示教学代价、结构复杂度和形式化坐标解码器类的精确容量分离开来。通过版本空间语义显式处理背景知识和开放/封闭世界假设，证明了有限开放世界提示下的完成三分性定理，刻画了开放世界教学数，并利用坐标-序等价得到了表示边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文学习通常被形式化为从函数示例中进行推理，但偏序集同时具有传递性、反对称性和不可比性，有限的提示可能无法确定查询的比较结果。现有工作缺乏对偏序集上上下文学习的系统理论分析，特别是在逻辑可识别性、教学代价、结构复杂度和模型容量等方面。

Method: 采用版本空间语义显式建模背景知识与开放/封闭世界假设；对有限开放世界提示（包含正反比较）证明完成三分性定理；通过覆盖数与阻塞集命中数刻画开放世界教学数；形式化依赖提示的s-坐标解码器，并利用经典坐标-序等价导出表示边界。

Result: 三分性：查询被强制为真、强制为假（因任何真完成都会产生环或违反反例）或真正歧义。开放世界教学数等于覆盖数加上阻塞集命中数，在n元偏序集上的最大值为n(n-1)，由反链唯一达到；阻塞项体现了开放世界语义相比完全哈斯语义的额外代价。表示边界：维数≤s是必要且充分的，宽度≤s是便捷的充分条件。

Conclusion: 该理论成功分离了偏序集上上下文学习的多个核心方面，提供了精确的完成三分性、教学代价和表示容量刻画，为理解结构关系上的上下文学习奠定了基础。

Abstract: In-context learning is commonly formalized as inference from examples of a function. Partial orders instead combine transitivity, antisymmetry, and incomparability, so a finite prompt may not determine a queried comparison. We develop a theory of in-context learning on partial orders that separates logical identifiability, prompt teaching cost, structural complexity, and the exact capacity of a formal coordinate-decoder class. A version-space semantics makes background knowledge and open- versus closed-world assumptions explicit. For finite open-world prompts with positive and negative comparisons, we prove an exact completion trichotomy: after taking the reflexive transitive closure of the positive demonstrations, a query is forced true, forced false because every true completion creates a cycle or violates a negative demonstration, or remains genuinely ambiguous. For a known $n$-element universe, we characterize the open-world teaching number as the number of covers plus a blocker-set hitting number, prove that its maximum over all $n$-element posets is $n(n-1)$ and is uniquely attained by the antichain, and identify the blocker term as the exact cost of open-world rather than complete-Hasse semantics. We formalize prompt-dependent $s$-coordinate decoders and use the classical coordinate-order equivalence to obtain an exact representation boundary: dimension at most $s$ is necessary and sufficient, while width at most $s$ is a convenient sufficient condition.

</details>


### [132] [When Does More Correct Data Hurt? Insertion-Stability and the Limits of Dimension-Based Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14020)
*Joseph Sankoorikal Johny*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在单调对抗者（monotone adversary）插入额外正确标记样本时，学习算法的鲁棒性。提出“插入稳定”（insertion-stable）学习器的概念，该类学习器在额外正确数据加入时不会增加错误区域，因此对对抗插入免疫。研究表明，并非所有类别都会受到惩罚，关键在于学习器是否与类别匹配。经典复杂度量（如VC维、Littlestone维）无法预测这种免疫性。对于某些类别，即使VC维相同，学习率也可能不同。此外，证明在Mehrotra的困难类别上，任何有限大小的单调置换不变压缩方案都无法达到清洁数据下的学习率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为添加正确数据总是安全的，但Larsen等人通过单调对抗者模型表明并非如此：对抗者可以读取i.i.d.训练样本并附加任意多个正确标记的示例，导致学习器性能下降。Mehrotra进一步证明了对于VC维d>=2的类别，期望误差的下界为Theta((d/n)log(en/d))，比清洁PAC率多一个对数因子。然而，这个下界是针对所有类别的坏情况，无法说明哪些类别实际遭受惩罚。因此，需要理解哪些学习器或类别能够避免这种惩罚。

Method: 定义“插入稳定”学习器：当输入更多正确标记样本时，其错误区域只会缩小。这种学习器对单调对抗者免疫，因为插入后风险不会超过清洁部分的风险。证明Closure（闭包）算法是插入稳定的，因此所有交集封闭（intersection-closed）类别都能保持清洁学习率E[Err] <= (21d+34)/n。通过构造具体例子，展示两个VC维和Littlestone维均为2的类别，一个学习率为Theta(1/n)，另一个为Theta(log(en)/n)，而区间（intervals）的Littlestone维无界却仍免疫。最后，在Mehrotra的困难类别上证明任何有限大小的单调置换不变压缩方案都无法达到清洁学习率。

Result: 插入稳定学习器对单调对抗者免疫，其风险不会因插入正确数据而增加。交集封闭类别通过Closure算法实现免疫，保持清洁学习率。经典维度（VC维、Littlestone维）无法预测免疫性：存在VC维和Littlestone维均为2的两个类别，一个免疫（Theta(1/n)），一个不免疫（Theta(log(en)/n)）；区间类Littlestone维无界但免疫。在Mehrotra的困难类别上，任何有限大小的单调置换不变压缩方案都无法达到清洁学习率，表明该困难不仅针对特定算法。

Conclusion: 问题不在于类别本身是否困难，也不在于学习器本身是否优秀，而在于二者是否匹配。如果存在一个在清洁数据上最优的插入稳定学习器，那么正确数据的添加是免费的；如果没有这样的学习器，那么代价属于类别，更换学习器也无法避免。因此，设计学习器时应考虑其对插入数据的稳定性，或者选择与插入稳定学习器匹配的类别。

Abstract: Adding data known to be correct ought to be safe. Not always. Larsen, Pabbaraju and Shetty model the failure with a monotone adversary, which reads an i.i.d. training sample and may append as many further examples as it likes, provided the target hypothesis labels them all. Mehrotra has since settled the cost, showing that for classes of VC dimension d >= 2 no learner can guarantee expected error better than Theta((d/n)log(en/d)), a logarithmic factor above the clean PAC rate.
  Because that rate is a worst case over all classes, it says nothing about which classes actually suffer the penalty, and the answer turns on the learner. We call a learner insertion-stable if feeding it more correctly labeled examples can only shrink the region where it errs. Such learners are immune to the adversary, since on any given sample the risk after insertions never exceeds the risk on the clean part alone, however much is added and however cleverly it is chosen. High- probability guarantees carry over unchanged, and because Closure is insertion-stable every intersection-closed class keeps its clean rate of E[Err] <= (21d+34)/n.
  Immunity is not something the classical dimensions can predict. Two classes can agree on VCdim = Ldim = 2 and still split, one at Theta(1/n) and the other at Theta(log(en)/n), while intervals have unbounded Littlestone dimension and are immune anyway. On Mehrotra's hard class we prove more than the failure of a single algorithm, showing that no monotone permutation-invariant compression scheme of any finite size attains the clean rate.
  The question is therefore not whether a class is hard, nor whether a learner is good, but whether the two suit each other. Given an insertion-stable learner that is optimal on clean data, correct additions are free, and without one the cost belongs to the class, so changing the learner will not avoid it.

</details>


### [133] [Adversarial Learning of Classifier-Free Guidance Schedules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14038)
*Ashwini Pokle,Alexandre Galashov,Arnaud Doucet,Mauricio Delbracio,Valentin De Bortoli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应的引导尺度调度方法，通过学习扩散时间、条件信息和当前噪声样本的函数来动态调整无分类器引导（CFG）的强度，以提升文本到图像生成的质量和对齐度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的无分类器引导（CFG）使用固定的全局引导尺度，这在不同时间步、样本和条件下是次优的，且可能引入伪影。虽然时间变化的调度已知能改善质量，但手工设计困难且依赖具体应用。

Method: 将动态引导调度问题转化为密度比估计问题：训练一个判别器来估计真实分布与引导边缘分布之间的时间相关对数密度比，同时一个轻量级生成器网络预测最优的、状态依赖的引导尺度。

Result: 该方法在文本到图像生成基准上优于启发式CFG调度以及先前的动态引导学习方法。

Conclusion: 通过密度比估计学习状态依赖的引导调度可以有效提升图像质量与文本对齐，避免了手工设计调度的困难。

Abstract: Modern text-to-image diffusion models rely on classifier-free guidance (CFG) to achieve high image fidelity and text alignment. However, CFG typically applies a static, global scale across all timesteps, samples, and conditions -- a choice that is generally suboptimal and can introduce artifacts, as different states may benefit from different levels of guidance. While time-varying schedules are known to improve quality, designing them by hand is non-trivial and application-dependent. In this paper, we learn the guidance schedule as a function of diffusion time, conditioning and the current noisy sample, in order to better align sampled images with the text prompt. We frame this as a density ratio estimation problem: a discriminator is trained to estimate the time-dependent log-density ratio between the true and guided marginal distributions, while a lightweight generator network predicts the optimal, state-dependent guidance scale. Empirically, our approach outperforms both heuristic CFG schedules and prior methods for learning dynamic guidance on text-to-image generation benchmarks.

</details>


### [134] [Model-agnostic Retrieval-Augmented Extended Forecasting for time series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14054)
*Juan Pablo Villa Serna,Rohan Asthana,Vasileios Belagiannis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种模型无关的时间序列预测方法RAEF（检索增强扩展预测），通过输入空间直接检索和拼接聚合改进检索增强生成框架，在避免微调计算开销的同时达到与其相当或更优的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练基础模型在时间序列零样本预测上表现强劲，但针对历史数据稀缺的领域特定任务通常需要微调或RAG适配。微调有效但计算成本高，本文探索更高效的RAG替代方案，旨在降低域适应开销的同时保持预测精度。

Method: 基于RAF提出RAEF，核心改进包括：(1) 在输入空间而非嵌入空间直接检索，降低推理开销；(2) 采用拼接聚合而非平均聚合，保留时序结构信息。RAEF是模型无关的方法，可即插即用于不同基础模型。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，RAEF在预测精度和推理开销两方面均优于RAF；与零样本和微调基础模型的全面对比显示，RAEF能达到与微调竞争甚至更优的性能，同时避免其计算负担。

Conclusion: RAEF作为一种实用且可扩展的域适应方法，在时间序列预测中无需微调即可获得高精度，为实际部署提供了高效替代方案。

Abstract: Time series forecasting with pretrained foundation models has demonstrated strong zero-shot capabilities. However, achieving optimal performance on time series with short or negligible historical data in domain-specific applications typically requires adaptation via either fine-tuning or RAG. While fine-tuning is effective, it incurs substantial computational costs. This work explores RAG within univariate time series (Retrieval Augmented Generation) as a more efficient alternative, in particular RAF (Retrieval Augmented Forecasting), and introduces RAEF (Retrieval-Augmented Extended Forecasting), a model-agnostic method built upon RAF. RAEF incorporates key refinements to the retrieval and aggregation mechanisms: (1) direct retrieval in input-space rather than embedding-space, reducing inference overhead, and (2) concatenation-based aggregation that preserves temporal structure instead of averaging. Empirical evaluation across multiple benchmark datasets demonstrates that RAEF outperforms RAF in both accuracy and inference overhead. Furthermore, comprehensive comparisons with zero-shot and fine-tuned foundation models show that RAEF achieves competitive or superior performance to fine-tuning while avoiding its computational burden, establishing it as a practical and scalable approach for domain adaptation in time series forecasting.

</details>


### [135] [When Denoising Hurts: Rethinking the Terminal Step of Diffusion Time Series Forecasters -- Extended Version](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14067)
*Dat Nguyen-Cong,Luong Tran,Tung Kieu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了扩散模型在时间序列预测中均匀有益的假设，发现高噪声阶段已恢复时间结构，而低噪声细化会引入统计漂移；据此提出无标签全局早停准则和伯努利时间步采样器，加速推理并提升精度，在八个数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型用于时序预测时，默认整个逆向扩散过程均有益，但实际中低噪声阶段的细化可能损害预测质量。作者旨在揭示这一现象并利用其优化推理与训练效率。

Method: 1) 分析预测质量随逆向扩散的演变，发现高噪声区恢复总体结构，低噪声区引入统计漂移；2) 提出无标签全局早停准则，动态确定最优终止点；3) 设计伯努利时间步采样器，在训练中集中采样高噪声区域同时保留完整过程覆盖。

Result: 在八个真实世界数据集上，所提方法在预测精度和推理速度上均优于现有扩散模型及其他基线方法。

Conclusion: 扩散模型在时序预测中并非所有逆向步骤都必要；通过早停和针对性的训练采样，可同时提升效率与准确性。

Abstract: Diffusion models offer a natural way to model uncertainty in time series forecasting, yet their iterative sampling process is often treated as a uniformly beneficial refinement procedure. Our study challenges this view by examining how forecast quality evolves throughout reverse diffusion. We find that general temporal structure is often recovered at relatively high noise levels, whereas continued low-noise refinement can introduce statistical drift and degrade the final forecast. Our analysis further suggests that this behavior explains why prior methods often favor relatively narrow diffusion architecture and schedule design. Building on this observation, we propose a label-free global stopping criterion that detects the optimal termination point, eventually speeding up inference and improving predictive accuracy. Additionally, since early stopping terminates inference in high-noise regions, we propose a Bernoulli timestep sampler that concentrates training on this region while preserving coverage of the full diffusion process. Extensive experiments conducted across eight real-world datasets demonstrate the superior performance of our method compared to existing approaches.

</details>


### [136] [Resource-Adaptive Primal-Dual Learning for One-Warehouse Multi-Store Systems with Censored Demand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14096)
*Jiameng Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种资源自适应原始-对偶学习框架（Resource-Adaptive Primal-Dual Learning），用于单仓库多商店（OWMS）库存系统，通过跟踪剩余资源状态下的原始-对偶求解路径，利用审查销售数据作为梯度估计，实现了对数期望遗憾，优于现有平方根阶的遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的OWMS学习策略基于固定目标（校准到初始平均资源率），但无法在实现销售改变剩余资源时重新调整中心。需要一种能够自适应资源状态变化的学习策略。

Method: 提出Resource-Adaptive Primal-Dual Learning框架，在每个时期，当前资源率索引目标商店分配和对偶变量，审查销售提供梯度估计用于更新二者。分析结合期望销售几何与移动目标论证，导出对数期望遗憾。

Result: 实现了对数期望遗憾，改进了现有OWMS学习策略的平方根阶保证。数值实验在不同时间范围和库存体制下展示了良好性能。

Conclusion: 该框架的设计和分析思路可为其他涉及共享资源耗尽的在线学习问题提供参考。

Abstract: The one-warehouse multi-store (OWMS) system is a fundamental inventory network in which a nonreplenishable warehouse allocates shared stock across multiple stores over time. Existing OWMS learning policies are built around a fixed target calibrated to the initial average resource rate, but such a fixed-target architecture cannot re-center after realized sales change the remaining resource available per future period. We develop Resource-Adaptive Primal-Dual Learning, a new learning framework that tracks the primal-dual resolving path with censored demand as the remaining-resource state evolves. In each period, the current resource rate indexes the target store allocations and dual variable, while censored sales provide gradient estimates for updating both. The analysis combines expected-sales geometry with a moving-target argument to yield logarithmic expected regret, improving on the state-of-the-art square-root-order guarantees of existing OWMS learning policies. The underlying design and analytical ideas may inform other online learning problems with depleting shared resources. Numerical experiments further demonstrate good finite-horizon performance of a practical variant across different horizon lengths and inventory regimes.

</details>


### [137] [Sequence prediction under a lying oracle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14102)
*Puspabeethi Samanta,Nikhil Karamchandani,Jayakrishnan Nair*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在存在说谎预言机的情况下，通过比较查询进行序贯预测的问题。作者针对随机环境和对抗环境分别提出了算法，并证明了它们的遗憾值具有对数上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统预测问题假设预测成本与概率分配直接相关，但本文考虑的场景中，预测是通过向一个可能说谎的预言机进行“比较查询”来实现的，这引入了额外的复杂性。因此，需要设计能够在这种“说谎预言机”环境下有效学习的算法。

Method: 对于随机环境，设计了基于似然估计的算法；对于对抗环境，采用了类似指数梯度下降或在线凸优化的方法。两种算法均通过调整预测分布来最小化累积遗憾。

Result: 在随机环境和对抗环境下，所提算法的遗憾值均达到对数级别上界，即 regret = O(log T)，其中 T 是时间步数。

Conclusion: 本文证明了即使存在说谎预言机，通过适当的算法设计仍能实现对数遗憾，表明该预测问题在理论上可解且高效。

Abstract: We consider the problem of sequential prediction of an $m$-ary sequence, where at each epoch, (i) the environment selects an outcome from an $m$-ary alphabet, (ii) the learner selects a probability distribution over the same alphabet (unaware of the outcome generated by the environment), and finally, (iii) the learner incurs a cost that depends on the probability assigned to the outcome. The cost function we consider captures the complexity of predicting the outcome generated by the environment, in a scenario where the aforementioned prediction is performed via comparative queries to a lying oracle. We consider both stochastic and adversarial environments, propose algorithms for both settings, and establish logarithmic upper bounds on their regret.

</details>


### [138] [Forecast Collapse in Time-Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14106)
*Shu Wan,Miles Ma,Hank Zhu,Guangqi Liu,Stephen Wang,Qingsong Wen,Huan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现了在预测股票小时收益率时出现的“预测崩溃”现象：预测值近乎平坦，排序能力差（横截面相关性低）。而在同样设置下预测交易量时该现象消失。作者系统研究了多种时间序列模型，发现预测崩溃与目标可预测性密切相关，并指出两个原因：低可预测性限制了点预测的幅度，以及单序列目标函数忽略了跨序列结构。进一步揭示了校准-排序权衡：优化均方误差导致平坦预测，而直接优化横截面相关性可提升排序但会过度放大预测幅度。为此提出CalibRank目标函数，平衡校准与排序。在Finance1K上，CalibRank使横截面相关性近乎翻三倍，同时保持幅度接近真实目标，并在所有测试模型上提升了相关性。结果揭示了传统时间序列评估的盲点：单序列指标可能掩盖跨序列结构的失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在预测1000只美国股票的每小时收益率时，观察到预测值变得几乎平坦且股票排序能力差（横截面相关性低）的意外现象（称为“预测崩溃”），而预测交易量时该现象基本消失。这促使作者深入探究预测崩溃的原因及其与目标可预测性的关系，并发现了校准与排序之间的权衡问题。

Method: 在多种时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）、12个深度学习预测模型和97个公开基准配置上系统研究预测崩溃现象。识别出两个原因：低可预测性限制了校准点预测的幅度，而单序列目标函数未识别跨序列结构。提出CalibRank目标函数，平衡均方误差（校准）和横截面相关性（排序）。

Result: 在Finance1K数据集上，CalibRank几乎将横截面相关性提高了三倍，同时保持预测幅度接近真实目标；在所有测试模型上均提升了相关性。而直接优化横截面相关性虽能提升排序，但会使预测幅度扩大一个数量级以上。

Conclusion: 传统时间序列评估存在盲点：单序列指标（如均方误差）可能隐藏跨序列结构的失败，而跨序列结构对下游决策至关重要。校准-排序权衡是实际应用中的关键问题，CalibRank提供了一种有效的平衡方案。

Abstract: When forecasting hourly returns for 1,000 US equities, we observe an unexpected phenomenon: predictions become nearly flat and show poor stock ranking, as measured by cross-sectional correlation. We call this forecast collapse. Surprisingly, the phenomenon largely disappears when forecasting trading volume under the same setting. We investigate forecast collapse across time-series foundation models (TSFMs), twelve deep-learning forecasting models, and 97 public benchmark configurations, and find that it is closely tied to target predictability. We identify two distinct reasons behind it: low predictability limits the amplitude of calibrated point forecasts, while per-series objectives leave cross-series structure unidentified. These findings reveal a calibration-ranking tradeoff: optimizing squared error leads to flat predictions, whereas directly optimizing cross-sectional correlation improves ranking but can inflate forecast amplitude by more than an order of magnitude. To address this tradeoff, we introduce CalibRank, a simple objective that balances calibration and ranking. On Finance1K, CalibRank nearly triples cross-sectional correlation while keeping amplitude close to the target, and improves correlation on all tested models. Our results reveal a blind spot in conventional time-series evaluation: per-series metrics can hide failures in cross-series structure needed by downstream decisions.

</details>


### [139] [Learning to Run Power Networks: Effective AlphaZero-inspired Topological Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14114)
*Lukas Zetto,Benjamin Schäfer,Qiong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了基于AlphaZero的强化学习方法在电力电网拓扑重构中的有效性，通过Monte Carlo Tree Search进行主动电网管理。优化后的AlphaZero实现了98.43%的存活率，显著优于PPO。研究发现，无先验策略/价值函数引导的MCTS可提升训练效率，二元存活奖励比复杂多目标函数更有效。结论是纯RL不足，需要“极简”融合领域启发式、二元奖励和受限线路负载观测空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着不稳定可再生能源整合增加电网压力，强化学习用于自主拓扑重构成为有前景的研究领域。拓扑动作比传统重新调度更便宜、更经济，但面临巨大组合动作空间和严格运行约束的挑战。

Method: 采用基于AlphaZero的模型驱动方法，利用Monte Carlo Tree Search进行主动电网管理。系统评估奖励函数、观测密度和搜索引导对智能体存活率的影响。对比优化后的AlphaZero与PPO变体。

Result: 优化后的AlphaZero达到98.43%峰值存活率，显著优于PPO。不带先验策略/价值函数引导的MCTS可提升训练效率；二元生存奖励比复杂多目标奖励提供更有效的搜索引导。

Conclusion: AlphaZero是拓扑控制的强大框架，但纯强化学习不足；有效可靠系统需要“极简”整合领域特定启发式、二元奖励和受限的线路负载观测空间。

Abstract: As the integration of volatile renewable energy sources increases the strain on modern power grids, the use of Reinforcement Learning (RL) for autonomous topological reconfiguration has emerged as a promising research field to keep strained grids stable and operational. Compared to traditional redispatching measures, topological actions offer a cheaper and more cost-effective way to manage grid congestion. However, their implementation is hindered by a vast combinatorial action space and strict operational constraints. This paper investigates the effectiveness of model-based AlphaZero-inspired approaches that utilize Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) for proactive grid management. We systematically evaluate how reward functions, observation density, and search guidance influence an agent's survivability. Our results demonstrate that the optimized AlphaZero approach achieves a peak survivability of 98.43%, significantly outperforming the proximal policy optimization (PPO) variant. We find that conducting the MCTS without guidance from a prior learned policy or value function can enhance training efficiency, and that a straightforward binary survival reward provides more effective search guidance than complex, multi-objective functions. Our findings demonstrate that while AlphaZero is a powerful framework for topological control, pure reinforcement learning is not sufficient; rather, an effective and reliable system requires a 'minimalist' integration of domain-specific heuristics, binary rewards, and a restricted observation space of line loads.

</details>


### [140] [From Fixed Grids to Moving Particles:A Transferable Latent Operator for Fluid Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14120)
*Meng Li,Chuqi Chen,Zhengqing Gao,Xi Zhou,Xiao Sun,Yang Xiang,Huaxi Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可迁移潜算子（TLO），该模型仅使用欧拉观测训练，无需拉格朗日监督或任务特定适应，即可零样本泛化到拉格朗日粒子轨迹预测，并在多个流体动力学基准上优于现有神经算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉格朗日轨迹（粒子输运）比欧拉场更难获得，而现有神经算子主要训练和评估于欧拉表示，导致模型无法直接泛化到拉格朗日任务。因此，需要一种仅从欧拉观测学习、却能零样本预测拉格朗日粒子轨迹的方法。

Method: 提出可迁移潜算子（TLO），学习欧拉场预测和拉格朗日粒子轨迹共享的统一流表示。TLO将潜在流演化与坐标相关解码解耦：在固定空间坐标查询演化潜表示得到欧拉场；在粒子位置查询速度并递归更新位置实现拉格朗日 rollout。

Result: 在五个流体动力学基准上，TLO在欧拉场预测和零样本拉格朗日 rollout 中均一致优于现有神经算子，且通过有限拉格朗日微调可进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: TLO成功实现了从欧拉观测到拉格朗日轨迹的零样本泛化，弥合了两种表示的鸿沟，为流体动力学中缺乏拉格朗日数据的情况提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Lagrangian modeling is vital to fluid dynamics, as it characterizes particle transport and complements the Eulerian representation. However, Lagrangian trajectories are less commonly available than Eulerian fields, while most neural operators are trained and evaluated primarily in the Eulerian representation. This mismatch motivates a new learning problem: can a model trained solely on Eulerian observations generalize zero-shot from Eulerian field prediction to Lagrangian particle rollout, without Lagrangian supervision or task-specific adaptation? To address this problem, we propose the Transferable Latent Operator (TLO), which learns a unified flow representation shared by Eulerian field prediction and Lagrangian particle rollout. TLO decouples latent flow evolution from coordinate-dependent decoding: querying the evolving latent representation at fixed spatial coordinates yields Eulerian fields, whereas querying velocities at particle positions and recursively updating these positions enables Lagrangian rollout. Across five fluid-dynamics benchmarks, TLO consistently outperforms existing neural operators in both Eulerian field prediction and zero-shot Lagrangian rollout, with further gains from limited Lagrangian fine-tuning.

</details>


### [141] [Overcoming Shortcut Learning in Graph Neural Networks through Active Explanation Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14121)
*Taraneh Younesian,Steve Azzolin,Antonio Longa,Francesco Ferrini,Vincenzo Marco De Luca,Stefano Teso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出XIGL，一种架构无关的“人在环”策略，通过检测和纠正GNN中的捷径（spurious correlations）来提升其在分布外任务中的可靠性。核心思路是：通过解释检测捷径，再由专家提供纠正反馈以去混淆模型。为降低标注成本，采用主动学习优先筛选最可能显示捷径行为的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN可能无意中利用捷径（即与预测相关但非因果的边、节点、特征）来完成预测任务，这损害了其在分布外任务中的可靠性。现有方法缺乏针对GNN捷径问题的系统性、架构无关的干预手段。

Method: 提出XIGL，一种架构无关的“人在环”策略，包含两步：(1) 通过检查GNN解释来检测捷径依赖；(2) 专家用户提供定制化纠正反馈以去混淆模型。支持任意查询策略，并开发主动学习策略优先筛选最可能显示捷径行为的解释以降低标注成本。

Result: 在多种GNN架构上验证了XIGL的有效性，包括现有和提出的基于解释的策略。代码已开源。

Conclusion: XIGL能有效去除GNN中的捷径，提升模型在分布外任务中的可靠性，并且通过主动学习降低了人工反馈成本，是一种实用且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) can solve prediction tasks by unintentionally exploiting shortcuts---that is, edges, nodes, and features that correlate with but are not causal for the prediction---which compromise their reliability in out-of-distribution tasks. We introduce XIGL, an architecture-agnostic human-in-the-loop strategy for removing such shortcuts from GNNs. Our key insight is twofold. On the one hand, reliance on shortcuts can be detected by inspecting GNN explanations. On the other hand, once made aware of such shortcuts, sufficiently expert users can provide tailored corrective feedback, which helps deconfound the model. XIGL supports any query strategy; however, since corrective feedback can be expensive to acquire, we develop an active learning strategy for prioritizing explanations that are more likely to display shortcut behavior, lowering annotation and cognitive costs. We showcase the effectiveness of XIGL, including both existing and proposed explanation-based strategies, on several GNN architectures. Our implementation is available online.

</details>


### [142] [Smart routes: a system for development and comparison of algorithms for solving vehicle routing problems with realistic constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14140)
*Andrew Soroka,German Mikhelson,Alexander Mescheryakov,Sergey Gerasimov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对带时间窗的容量车辆路径问题（CVRPTW），对比了精确求解器SCIP与多种启发式算法（LKH、2-OPT、3-OPT、ORTools）及深度学习模型JAMPR的性能。实验表明，在问题规模为50时，深度学习和经典启发式解已接近SCIP精确解但耗时更少；规模为100时，SCIP比神经/经典启发式慢约13倍且成本相当，或在相同时间内首次可行解质量差约50%。作者还开发了集成多种算法的Smart Routes平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着全球城市人口增长，带现实约束的路径优化问题愈发重要。尽管存在理论上能提供精确最优解的方法，但随着问题规模增大，其指数级复杂度使得应用变得困难，亟需高效的近似解法。

Method: 针对CVRPTW问题，使用精确求解器SCIP作为基准，对比了经典启发式算法（LKH、2-OPT、3-OPT、ORTools框架）和深度学习模型JAMPR。作者开发了Smart Routes平台来集成这些算法并开展实验。

Result: 规模50时，深度学习和经典启发式解与SCIP精确解接近且耗时更少。规模100时，SCIP比神经/经典启发式慢约13倍且路线成本相同；若限定相同时间，SCIP的首次可行解比启发式差约50%。

Conclusion: 在大规模CVRPTW问题中，深度学习和经典启发式算法能在可接受时间内获得与精确解相当的质量，而精确求解器在效率上明显不足。Smart Routes平台为路径优化研究提供了实用的集成工具。

Abstract: The problem of route optimization with realistic constraints is becoming extremely relevant in the face of global urban population growth. While we are aware of approaches that theoretically provide an exact optimal solution, their application becomes challenging as the problem size increases because of exponential complexity.
